- Caution (Claude vetting): NOVA invented must-do dates ("2026-Q1 試產, 2025-Q4 完成 ERP"), scripted openers that congratulate the customer on its capital increase and equipment (quoting filings, §43) and that assert "寧波母公司本身也是 T100 的好夥伴" before any check, named the possible parent's US buyer (Tractor Supply) and its audits to the customer (the buyer link was the name-match company's, not the plant's), used "鉅額罰款" and "數億泰銖" stuck working capital (unsourced, fear framing), offered a "泰國中資企業 T100 本地化成功案例" deck (none verified), treated BOI as given, and proposed CRM keywords with guessed characters (恒克斯, 汉克斯, 恒格斯). None adopted. A genuine one-hour Thai tax session for the GM or finance head, offered by the field owner, was kept. No control-workaround, recording or direct-contact advice was offered.

## §135 — PRE-BUILD Thai plant company (registered 2025-08, ฿5M → ฿150M, about ฿1.25M paid in, no PP&E, stock or sales) of a PRC-LISTED connector group (SSE STAR, ~RMB 3.15B revenue, EV-led, adding AI data-centre copper) whose annual report states "公司与各子公司均统一使用 ERP、PLM、MES 等系统" (brand not public); held via a Singapore company (99.50) plus a 0.50 Chinese holder; the parent's chairman and CFO are Thai directors, any one signs with the seal; the half-year report still says "筹建规划" and re-describes the Thai company as AI-computing connection components; ACP D whose only report is an invitation to a BOI online seminar to a data-list 經理; lead in a colleague's 私田 (NOVA live 2026-10-25 · job 0ccb90b4 · RECODEAL INTERCONNECT SYSTEM (THAILAND) run · 南高-商機助手)
- **Grade:** an event invitation with no needs conversation = **E (公田/長期經營)** by the behaviour rule (one notch below the SDR's D; recorded, not changed). Same reading as §128 (pre-build) and stricter than §77 (D). Lift to C2: a Thai director (e.g. the CFO) or a seconded build lead replies and agrees to a one-to-one on the Thai plant's IT plan, raises a concrete issue (how the group ERP lands in Thailand, Thai tax or BOI reporting) and names a Thai finance or IT counterpart. C1 = the 立案三要項.
- **Entry ranking when the parent writes a unified-systems policy into its annual report (NOVA's ranking, a prior):** 1) Thai localisation (Thai documents, VAT, WHT, e-Tax where wanted) beside or on the group ERP; 2) BOI/customs records, only once promotion is confirmed; 3) WMS + barcode at production start, only if HQ lets Thailand choose; "do nothing" lowest. Position as the group system's Thai landing partner, never against HQ's choice (consistent with §22, §69, §81, §128). If the group ERP is a DigiWin product, the play becomes in-family extension (§43, §61, §79).
- **Parent status check:** NOVA did not know whether the parent is a 鼎捷 account or its ERP brand, and said so. The check is a CRM search on the parent's full Chinese name and USCC plus the DigiWin China office for the parent's city; per the run rules it is routed by the field owner, not by the channel head.
- **Connector / high-speed-copper greenfield discovery themes (questions, not claims):** does Suzhou IT copy the group ERP/PLM/MES to Thailand and support it remotely, or may Thailand choose local tax or warehouse systems; how Thai books, VAT and WHT are kept today (accounting firm, group system, nothing yet); whether BOI was applied for; building, equipment and trial-production months; first product line (automotive harness connectors, storage busbars or data-centre copper); who approves Thai system selection and acceptance (Thai directors or Suzhou).
- Caution (Claude vetting): NOVA told the channel head to contact the DigiWin China Suzhou AM himself (left to the field owner per the run rules); claimed global ERPs are weak at Thai tax and that DigiWin TH has a ready localisation add-on for any ERP (unsourced; needs TH product confirmation before any promise); called BOI "極可能" and said parent ERPs "通常不支援泰國 BOI 特殊關務" (BOI not established; unsourced); and read our own invitation as the customer "having engaged with BOI events" (no evidence they attended). None adopted. No control-workaround, recording or pretext advice was offered.

## §136 — BUILT, pre-production Thai flagship plant (registered 2023-09, ฿668M registered after a 2026-09 raise, PP&E ~฿419M on a LEASED site, FY2025 loss ~฿159M on service-type income, equity negative, ~฿603M group-funded current liabilities) of a US NASDAQ-LISTED power-electronics group (~USD 1.8B revenue) whose 10-K describes a phased global ERP programme and whose Thai job posts name SAP S/4HANA, SAP IBP, SAP EWM, SAP MDG, an "approved" MES and PLM as the plant's working systems; two Thai directors carry the names of the parent's EVP & COO and EVP & General Counsel, any one signs; first production revenue due Q4 2026; ACP D whose only report is "展会认识了解MES，正在找机会去拜访" from a data-list 副總裁 who is not a director; lead in a colleague's 私田 (NOVA live 2026-10-25 · job da72da17 · ADVANCED ENERGY INDUSTRIES (THAILAND) run · 南高-商機助手)
- **Grade:** a trade-show MES question with no two-way needs talk, no 立案三要項, at a plant whose own job posts staff the group's SAP and MES = **E (公田/長期經營)**, one notch below the SDR's D (recorded, not changed). Same reading as §104 (named SAP + MES running). An MES question during a group-SAP roll-out is most likely technical scouting until Thailand's own buying authority is confirmed.
- **Entry ranking when the parent's own posts name SAP S/4HANA + IBP + EWM and an approved MES (NOVA's ranking, a prior):** 1) monitor only ("不要跟鬼打架": the group owns the IT architecture); 2) shop-floor equipment and test-data collection feeding the group MES/SAP, a weak chance and ONLY with explicit group-IT delegation (interfaces per §22); 3) Thai e-Tax/tax-filing edges around SAP, limited room. ERP, WMS (SAP EWM) and APS (SAP IBP) are closed; do not propose them.
- **Lift to C2 (observable events):** the contact or a Thai IT lead states that group IT has authorised Thailand to find a third-party tool for specific equipment or test data and is evaluating; or the customer volunteers that the group's EWM or MES timing cannot meet the production start. Plus a named Thai owner. C1 = the 立案三要項.
- **First-visit prompts (questions, not claims):** what exactly he wanted from MES (equipment data, test and traceability, or the timing of the group system's arrival); whether Thailand's MES is a group standard deployed centrally or whether Thailand may evaluate third-party tools in a defined scope; the biggest operating risk at first volume; who the Thai project owner would be.
- **Reusable OSINT method:** for a multinational's new plant, read every body on the group careers portal filtered to Thailand: duty sentences ("maintain … data in SAP IBP, SAP S/4HANA, MES, WMS") are dated, first-party evidence of the plant's systems and settle the incumbent read faster than press or LinkedIn.
- Caution (Claude vetting): NOVA assumed the group MES is "SAP MES" and called SAP-side equipment integration very costly and slow (brand not established; unsourced); claimed a DigiWin local advantage in OT-protocol integration (not verified for TH); said Thai e-Tax is foreign companies' biggest headache and that the Big Four usually handle it (unsourced); framed rank 2 as a "專案切割" (a carve-out pitched to sidestep the group's approved-systems list is not adopted; a local scope is acceptable only when group IT itself delegates it); and suggested finding the new Thai IT lead on LinkedIn and talking to him (LinkedIn snippets only; any contact goes openly through the field owner). It said plainly it does not know the MES brand or any DigiWin relationship. No recording or pretext advice was offered.

## §137 — PRE-OPERATIONAL Thai BOI greenfield (registered 2026-02, ฿5M → ฿530.72M in nine weeks, fully paid, no statements) of a PRC family-controlled precision-screw and seat-drive maker in a ChiNext IPO INQUIRY round, held via BVI → Hong Kong 99 / Singapore 1; BOI approved a ฿2,120M robot-screw project six days after registration (province in the BOI release differs from the registered office); parent's ERP/MES brands not public, an HQ "AI and IT information department" on record; sole Thai director = the parent's chairman; ACP D whose only report is "attended our event, not yet met; factory address not confirmed; looking for another way to reach HQ"; contact = a data-list 經理 on a mainland consumer mailbox; lead in a colleague's 私田 (NOVA live 2026-10-25 · job 34b4870f · SEENPIN ELECTROMECHANICAL TRANSMISSION (THAILAND) run · 南高-商機助手)
- **Grade:** two event attendances with no meeting and no needs conversation = **D (需求探詢)** in NOVA's live reading (not E, unlike §128/§135 where the only touch was our invitation: actual attendance counts as the customer gathering information). Lift to C2: a first real meeting (online is enough) with the contact or the Thai build lead that confirms an IT-planning timeline, a BOI machinery-import or Thai-tax issue, and where the system decision sits (HQ IT, a Thai build lead, or the chairman). C1 = the 立案三要項.
- **First ticket at a BOI greenfield before construction:** BOI machinery (eMT) records tied to fixed assets plus Thai finance localisation for the build phase; a full ERP pitch before the building exists gets deferred ("廠房還沒蓋好"). Consistent with §39 (eMT 100% in phase 1, RMTS ready before trial production) and §135.
- **IPO-inquiry parent:** expect HQ to standardise subsidiary systems and to prefer either its own system's branch edition or a product with cross-border consolidation and local service (NOVA; direction consistent with §74 and §128). Position as the Thai landing layer that fits HQ's standard.
- **Route:** the SDR (or her leader) first checks DigiWin's CRM for the parent; the SDR's leader then asks DigiWin China's office for the parent's province through the overseas-business liaison what the parent runs and whether it is an account. The channel head does not drive this himself on a colleague's account.
- **Precision screw / seat-drive new-plant discovery themes (questions only):** lot traceability from bar stock through thread grinding and heat treatment to the finished screw (IATF lines at the parent); how BOM and engineering changes reach the Thai floor from HQ R&D; how the BOI machinery register links to maintenance and utilisation of high-value grinders.
- **Reusable OSINT method (Claude, this run):** a PRC parent's IPO file list is public on the exchange's project API (SZSE `listing.szse.cn/api/ras/projectrends/query?keywords=<short name>` then `/details?id=<prjid>`); the prospectus's subsidiary table gives the Thai company's date, capital, paid-in, address and holders, which can be matched to DBD to prove the parent without a paid บอจ.5. The legal opinion's organisation list shows whether HQ has an IT department.
- Caution (Claude vetting): NOVA called the HQ stance "零容錯…SOX 運作" (absolute and unsourced for a ChiNext issuer); suggested the channel head push the overseas liaison himself (routing stays with the field owner); used illustrative C1 dates ("2026 年底前必須通過 IATF 16949", "2027 上半年投產前 ERP 必須上線") as if facts; scripted "一鍵式追溯", "即時同步" (beyond §22) and an unsourced "百萬級" equipment figure; built a question on a robot customer's "IATF 16949 廠審" (IATF is automotive); and called the lead a "指標大案". None adopted. No control-workaround, recording or pretext advice was offered.

## §138 — ACP D whose only daily report is "T100老客，国内用得不好。泰国还未上泰国在地财税模组。日常建联中" (contact = one data-list 經理, not a director; 25 days quiet; amount 0) at a RAMPING Thai export FURNITURE plant (registered 2025-01, ฿150M paid in, PP&E ~฿165M, first-year sales ~฿6.1M at a ~0 gross margin, net loss ~฿75M, stock ~฿88M ≈ 14× sales, ~฿262M interest-free current liabilities), 100 Singapore-held, two PRC-national directors either of whom signs; parent UNRESOLVED (name match with a HK-listed cross-border furniture e-commerce group whose filings report a self-owned factory in ramp-up, location unstated) (NOVA live 2026-10-25 · job 4f638b2a · AUGROUP INDUSTRY (AYUTTHAYA) run · 南高-商機助手)
- **Grade:** one courtesy-level report, a manager-level contact, no stated must-do, budget or timetable = **D (需求探詢)**. To C2: a power person (one of the Thai directors or the plant head) engaged and agreeing on a Thai operating/compliance pain. To C1: the 立案三要項 (a quantified must-do the customer states, a named project owner such as the Thai finance head or a PM, a dated go-live for the Thai finance/tax scope).
- **Reading "国内用得不好":** usually user-level complaints about cumbersome flows or customisations that never fitted, not proof the product is wrong. On the Thai side never agree with complaints about the group's T100; position Thai localisation as completing and protecting HQ's existing investment (Thai VAT/WHT, e-Tax invoices, Thai documents), not as a fix for the mainland. Whether and how DigiWin China's AM for the parent tests HQ IT/finance first is the field owner's call (NOVA: HQ IT holds the architecture veto; a Thai push that looks like bypassing HQ gets killed).
- **First cut for a ramping, stock-heavy plant on a group T100:** Thai finance localisation (VAT/WHT) + basic purchasing and stock with book-to-floor accuracy, plus BOI material/machinery records only if a certificate exists; defer barcode WMS and fine costing until the ramp settles. Consistent with §43, §76, §134.
- **Decision chain:** Thai directors = voice of the site pain and initiators of budget; HQ IT/finance = system-architecture veto. Field owner works the Thai directors/plant head; the parent's DigiWin China AM (arranged by the field owner's leader) handles HQ IT/finance.
- **Furniture proof point (verified in the TW case mirror):** case 3239 (2022-09-23) 井得工業越南: managers who used TIPTOP at the China plant, new Vietnam plant implemented Workflow ERP during set-up, outdoor furniture for US big-box buyers, cross-department data accuracy 60→90 (page claim). Malaysian/Vietnamese furniture makers (Wegmans 3133, Dee Noon 2076, 緯樹 1121, 凱勝 559) are ASEAN history; no Thai furniture case was verified.
- Caution (Claude vetting): NOVA called 井得 "Jinde" and said its China side ran "TIPTOP/T100" and that the case proves "跨國子母公司系統整合" (the page says TIPTOP at the China plant and a separate Workflow ERP in Vietnam, no integration claim); framed BOI as "補稅罰款極重" (fear framing, BOI status unknown); asserted "中資家具廠多為出口型" (unsourced); scripted a Thai accountant "天天用 Excel 手工報稅抓狂" (invented); suggested "泰國中資製造業" proof without a verified case. None adopted. Routing through DigiWin China is adopted only as the field owner's decision, never as Peter's direct contact. No control-workaround, recording or pretext advice was offered.

## §139 — ACP D whose only daily report is "HR系统+考勤系统在报价中" (HR plus attendance system being quoted; amount 0, 40 days quiet; contact from a data list whose name matches one of the two sole-signing DBD directors) at a profitable-then-loss-making Thai PRINTED-PAPER-PACKAGING plant (rigid gift boxes, folding cartons, molded fiber; ~฿348M sales, +53 in 2025 but a gross LOSS, SG&A ×2.2, stock ×4.9, receivables ~118 days, capital raised ฿180M → ฿320M in 2026-02) that self-describes as part of a Kunshan packaging group which runs DigiWin China T100 (+ smart logistics, BPM, BI; project launched 2020-07-15; on DigiWin China's 2025 print-and-packaging customer slide), Seychelles 100 holding, parent unproven by registry (NOVA live 2026-10-25 · job 8b8643fc · LIHUA ENVIRONMENTAL TECHNOLOGY (THAILAND) run · 南高-商機助手)
- **Grade:** one "quote in progress" report, no confirmed role for the contact, no amount, no must-do, no date = **D (需求探詢)**. To C2: the contact's authority confirmed and one of the two directors engaged and supporting the HR project. To C1: the 立案三要項 (a quantified must-do such as monthly hours lost to manual attendance and attendance-bonus checks under a Monday-to-Saturday schedule with different office and factory rules, or a labour-inspection risk; a stated budget range; a committed go-live month).
- **Positioning ("以小帶大"):** HR + attendance is the land; the proposal should carry a one-page Phase 2 view of the Thai plant on the group's T100 (Thai finance/tax localisation, Thai-site users, job cost) so a local HR vendor does not take the account by default. Decision split (hypothesis): HR + attendance = the Thai directors; T100/MES for Thailand = head-office IT and finance (the Thai plant cannot change the group ERP architecture alone). Consistent with §43, §76, §138.
- **HR scope honesty line:** per NOVA, DigiWin TH's HR offer covers attendance-device import, shift and OT rules with approvals, attendance-bonus rules by job group, and basic pay arithmetic; Thai social security and 50 ทวิ withholding stay with a Thai payroll package unless a current Thai localisation pack is confirmed. Same line as §95. Verify scope with DigiWin TH delivery in writing before any customer claim.
- **Second-phase wedge for a printed-packaging plant whose margin collapsed on fast growth:** quote-to-actual job cost and stock (raw board, WIP, finished goods) first; ask how the plant compares its estimate with the actual job cost, never quote its filing back at it.
- **In-family reference:** the group's own T100 (and MES, per DigiWin China's slide) is the strongest proof point, but only after the field owner's leader has the DigiWin China account manager for the parent confirm current use and maintenance status (case currency rule). Peter never contacts DigiWin China or the customer himself.
- Caution (Claude vetting): NOVA's capability list for the Thai HR product is unverified and its "確保 100 符合 SSO 與 50 ทวิ" is an overclaim (not adopted); its first-visit questions quoted the filing at the customer (capital raise, +50 growth, "數千萬存貨") and claimed "我們服務許多跨國印包廠" without a verified case list (both reworded); it assumed the contact is an HR person (the ACP contact's name matches a director); "the customer may already be talking to a Thai HR vendor such as TigerSoft" is a hypothesis, not a fact. No control-workaround, pretext or recording advice was offered.

## §140 — ACP D at a JAPANESE OEM tier-1 assembly plant (ex-Honda Lock Thai, Amata City; door mirrors, outside handles, door cylinders, ABS wheel sensors; ~฿3.8B, ~9.5 net margin, no interest-bearing debt, pays more than its profit to the parent each year) owned since 2023 by a TSE-listed components group (MinebeaMitsumi, via Minebea AccessSolutions, Miyazaki); the SDR's only report: "customer said MES need, materials sent, was keen, no reply for 2 months"; new-model hiring and a PP&E rise; lead in a colleague's 私田 (NOVA live 2026-10-25 · job c0b68d34 · MINEBEA ACCESSSOLUTIONS THAI run · 南高-商機助手)
- **Stage:** a stated MES need + two months of silence + no 立案三要項 = **D (需求探詢), close to E**. C2 comes back when a manufacturing or QA manager (or a director) confirms a digital intent tied to the new-model launch or a carmaker audit; C1 needs a quantified must-do, a named project window (e.g. IT + production section heads) and a go-live date.
- **Decision rights in a Japanese MNC group (NOVA's rule of thumb, verify per plant):** ERP is usually a parent/group standard the Thai plant cannot change; MES is often decided locally because it binds to local machines, fixtures and the carmakers' local audits. Verify with one question: were past shop-floor and poka-yoke systems built by the Thai IT team or approved in the Japanese head office? Never position a deal to bypass group IT approval.
- **Product and first cut:** NOVA routes OEM-tier-1 automotive MES to 鼎華 iMES's automotive template if DigiWin TH can deliver it locally (routing hint, confirm with TH management, as §72). First cut = ONE multi-part assembly line (door mirror or outside handle), values = part-level traceability (part, operator, component lots, key check values) and pass-station error-proofing; tie it to the new-model launch.
- **Wake-up (field owner only):** value-first note, English first with Japanese for Japanese executives, framed on the new-model launch and carmaker requirements, never a feature list; prepare a scenario demo (assembly-line pass-station error-proofing), never an empty demo; E after two channels and ten working days without an answer.
- Caution (Claude vetting): NOVA was told no contact title was recorded; the ACP contact record actually shows a 副總 whose name matches a sole-signing DBD director, so read the contact record before asking. Not adopted: LinkedIn as a channel (run rule), an illustrative "carmaker requires 100 barcode poka-yoke by 2027/Q1" stated like a fact, injection-moulding machines (not evidenced), a "1-page case study on Japanese tier-1 plants passing carmaker audits" (no such DigiWin case in the TW mirror or Monk cards; the nearest is 記銘工業, case 626, an aftermarket door-mirror maker, page 2018), a "100 poka-yoke" subject line, and competitor lines claiming 24/7 support, full EN/TH/JA screens and seconds-level control (unverified). No control-workaround, pretext or recording advice was offered.

## §141 — ACP D whose daily reports say the Thai plant BOUGHT YONYOU in 2025, runs it, "短时间不会更换，长期跟进", then "有其他人在跟，刪除" and a handover to another SDR; the only DigiWin touch an MES-event invitation; at a RAMPING Thai INVESTMENT-CASTING (lost-wax) and machining plant (registered 2022, ~฿532M PP&E, first sales year ~฿56M at cost 1.82× sales, stock ~317 days of COGS, receivables ~213 days, capital raised to ~฿738M, interest-light group funding), 100 Chinese capital, self-described affiliate of a Jiangsu foundry, IATF-led QC/QA Manager being hired; investment matters signed by one director alone; lead in a colleague's 私田 (NOVA live 2026-10-25 · job 47266452 · XINYU PRECISION (THAILAND) run · 南高-商機助手)
- **Grade:** a customer who has just bought a PRC-brand ERP and says he will not change = **E (長期經營)**; keeping D is "虛火" that misleads the funnel. Up to D when the customer himself admits a floor or traceability gap the ERP does not cover and asks how a DigiWin floor system connects to it; C2 when a power person (finance head or the investment signatory) adopts a stock or IATF goal and allows a floor survey. Reassigning the account (公田 or not) is the field owners' and their leaders' call.
- **Complement order beside a new Yonyou at a casting-machining plant:** eMES traceability first (wax and shell batch → pour/heat → casting → machining → finished part; rejects by process and cause), barcode WMS second (alloy, returns, WIP between foundry and CNC, finished parts). Position it as protecting the ERP investment, never as replacing it.
- **Interface claims (adopted):** may say the floor system passes completions, issues and scrap to the ERP through a standard interface or intermediate table; may NOT promise real-time two-way seamless sync, may NOT promise that the ERP side needs no work (that interface work is the customer's cost with the ERP vendor), may NOT take on support of the other vendor's ERP. Same rule as §22.
- **Watch, don't chase:** NOVA puts localisation trouble on a new PRC-brand ERP in Thailand at 6–12 months after go-live (first tax year, BOI audit, Thai staff turnover) — an unverified heuristic, use it only to time check-ins. Observable signals: JobThai/JobsDB posts for ERP-experienced IT staff or for BOI clerks who compile reports by hand, a full-capacity year that still shows a gross loss, an IATF external audit.
- **Investment-casting floor hypotheses (questions, never claims):** return-material (gates/risers) ratio in the melt; shell drying against Thai heat and humidity (sand inclusions, porosity, shell cracks); WIP piling between foundry and CNC with paper travellers; how long a reject found at machining takes to trace back to its pour.
- **Verified references for this process** (TW mirror, history only): 承鴻國際 (case 1080, page 2018, lost-wax foundry, Workflow ERP + SFT); 一原金屬 (case 3131, page 2022, valve castings-machining-assembly, sMES); 岡華企業 (case 965, page 2018, foundry turned machining, Smart ERP).
- Caution (Claude vetting): NOVA said the ERP "根本管不到" the floor (unverified competitor claim, internal only); scripted first-visit questions and an ice-breaker that quote the filing (stock days, capital raise) at the customer (§43; reworded); offered "release ฿18M by cutting WIP 20 percent" (unsourced); a "5-minute IATF trace" demo and audit test (invented benchmark); a free "2-hour TBB diagnosis" (not verified for DigiWin TH); two anonymous casting cases ("a large Chinese precision-casting leader 2021–2023, shell rejects −22", "a Taiwanese die-casting plant in Rayong 2022, WIP 120 → 78 days") not found in the TW mirror or the Monk cards; and LinkedIn as a hiring monitor (run rule: JobThai/JobsDB only). None adopted. No control-workaround, pretext, tax or recording advice was offered.

## §142 — Cold ACP D (two contacts, neither answers calls or accepts WeChat; only touch an event invitation) at a tiny FIRST-SALES-YEAR Thai plant (~฿7.6M at a gross loss, stock ~4× sales, ~฿270M PP&E on ~฿280M interest-free group liabilities) of a PRC-LISTED group that runs SAP at HQ (its ESG report) and names the Thai company in its annual report as a producing subsidiary ("special alloy project"); lead in a colleague's 私田 (NOVA live 2026-10-25 · job c9df90d3 · SHENGQUAN NEW MATERIAL (THAILAND) run · 南高-商機助手)
- **Grade:** contact data alone is not D. No conversation, no need, no budget = **E (公田 / 長期經營)**. D = a real conversation with a Thai-plant key person (finance manager, plant team) who voices a Thai-compliance or start-up concern; C2 = a power person (a director or the finance head) acknowledges a pain that has no internal consensus yet; C1 = the 立案三要項.
- **Positioning under a group SAP (two-tier):** group SAP keeps consolidation; DigiWin offers the Thai layer (Thai tax and statutory books; BOI only once a certificate is confirmed) and the plant floor (batch or heat records, lot stock), interfaced per project (§22). Never "replace SAP"; never sell "the cheapest basic package" to a listed group's plant; speak of phased compliance and a clean interface instead.
- **Batch-melting (alloy) floor themes, as questions only:** charge recipe versus actual charge per heat; charge and melt losses; off-spec heats; lot chemistry tied to what is shipped and to the certificate the customer receives; stock counted by lot.
- **Unreachable contacts:** the field owner's own legitimate moves only (her planned on-site visit with truthful identification; a value-first leaflet that really exists); the internal route to the parent comes first (ask DigiWin China's office in the parent's city whether the group is an account and what it runs).
- Caution (Claude vetting): NOVA called SAP "very hard to plug in" for Thai tax and BOI and warned of fines at start-up (overclaim, fear-selling, BOI unverified); said SAP's MES/WMS costs "極高" (unsourced); proposed LinkedIn searching for employees and WeChat-group "肉搜" (run rule and privacy; not adopted) and the SDR phoning the parent's division for the Thai team (left to the field owner); named a white paper not verified to exist; named references 源潤豐鑄造, 中策橡膠, 立中車輪 (not in the TW mirror or the Monk cards) and credited 勤美 (case 1185, 2018) with melt and COA automation the page does not describe (e-forms only). No verified alloy or foundry-consumables case exists in the mirror or the Monk cards (grep 2026-10-25); nearest verified chemical cases: 德亞樹脂 1059 (2018, resin, lot and shelf-life control), 三晃 999 (2018, PU resin and coatings, TIPTOP + EasyFlow), 大洋塗料 Vietnam 2044 (2019, ERP chosen before start-up).

## §143 — ACP D whose only daily report is "客户泰国告知需要ERP，但是不想一次性投入很大，考虑SAAS版本" (a customer-stated ERP need plus a budget-SHAPE preference; no amount, scope, date or titled contact) at a BUILT, equity-funded Thai brass-valve plant (registered 2024-05, ฿180M capital, PP&E ~฿147M, pre-revenue in its FY2025 filing, US bills of lading from 2026-04 to two US plumbing brands) held 99.72 from Singapore by an UNRESOLVED parent; lead in a colleague's 私田 (NOVA live 2026-10-25 · job f790d809 · HELBRI COPPER INDUSTRY (THAILAND) run · 南高-商機助手)
- **Grade:** a stated need with a budget-shape preference but no power person, no amount, no team and no date = **D (需求探詢)**, not C2. C2 = a meeting with the plant's real head (factory manager, GM or a director) who confirms willingness to improve operations; C1 = 立案三要項 (a quantified must-do, a named evaluation team from IT, finance or production control, and a go-live quarter). Consistent with §92, §107, §124; one notch below §100 (quote + budget + named shortlist = C2).
- **"No big one-off, want SaaS" (reusable):** answer with the subscription ERP shape, positioned on predictable multi-year cost of ownership and manufacturing fit, never on price per account; no price war with Kingdee or Yonyou cloud or local SaaS (same as §100). Phase 1 for a just-ramped export plant: buy, sell and stock (with export documents), AR/AP, Thai tax, and basic production reporting (issue and receipt) so material ties to finance; no APS, no automated WMS. BOI only if a certificate is confirmed.
- **Brass-valve discovery themes (NOVA's, questions only):** brass cost and chip (swarf) recovery offset against material cost; outsourced plating or heat treatment (stock held at the subcontractor, issue-versus-return differences, subcontract fees); US brand buyers' lot records (lead-free material certificates, pressure-test records). Claude vetting: NOVA's "chips are usually 30-50 of raw-material weight" and "usually 100 per cent pressure test" are unsourced and must not be quoted; whether plating is in-house (the registry objective mentions electroplating) or outsourced is itself a question.
- **Unknown parent behind a Singapore holder with a Chinese mail domain:** the field owner asks the contact directly and truthfully whether the group's IT or finance will join the evaluation; the customer's own answer or the paid DBD shareholder list (บอจ.5) identifies the holder. Any China-side lookup or cross-strait "Call High" runs through the field owner and her leader, never through the channel head.
- Caution (Claude vetting): NOVA framed C1 on "US buyer audit pressure or lose orders" (fear framing; audits not evidenced), put BOI duty-free filing in phase 1 (no BOI evidenced), claimed DigiWin can deliver "100 per cent" Thai tax compliance and that Kingdee and Yonyou "often need costly customisation" for Thai tax (overclaim; unverified competitor claim), used "buying wrong costs more than buying expensive" (fear framing), suggested probing the decision chain "under the pretext of case matching" (pretext; reworded to a direct question), told the channel head to engage DigiWin China's Zhejiang office himself (not adopted), and named reference cases 台州巨水銅業, 蘇爾達潔具 and 成霖企業 that are not in the TW case mirror or the Monk cards (not used). Verified valve and plumbing-hardware cases in the mirror: 一原金屬 3131 (2022, valves, value-delivery engagement, deck title ROI 5.3×), 登煬精密 3446 (2023, ball valves, SmartERP), 泉全閥業 782 (2018, valves, ERP), 資勇企業 758 (2018, die-cast and plated sanitary hardware, ERP + HRM), 凱撒衛浴 2471 (2020, sanitary ware in Vietnam, ERP via DigiWin Vietnam). No control-workaround or customer-recording advice was offered.

## §144 — ACP D whose only daily report is "加到了客户HR微信，但是对方告知公司访问SAP，不打算另买ERP" (an HR contact on WeChat names SAP and REFUSES another ERP; no finance, IT or plant contact; amount 0; 33 days quiet) at a RAMPING Thai PDC-cutter plant (registered 2023-05, ฿447M registered, ~฿313M paid in, PP&E ~฿397M, FY2025 first sales ~฿43.5M at a loss, stock ~3.7× cost of sales, ~฿328M interest-free group-funded current liabilities; profitable in H1 2026 per the parent) held 99.99 through Hong Kong by a NEEQ-LISTED Shenzhen superhard-materials parent in a BSE IPO review, whose GM and Deputy GM are the Thai directors and whose prospectus costs production in an unnamed "ERP 系统"; BOI-promoted; oilfield majors audit the plant; lead in a colleague's 私田 (NOVA live 2026-10-25 · job 025a9421 · LINKDIAMOND run · 南高-商機助手)
- **Grade:** an HR-relayed refusal with a named incumbent = **E (公田 / 長期經營)**, down from ACP D (same rule as §91, §104, §142: system facts or a refusal from a non-power contact, no pain, no amount). Back to C2 = through the field owner, a plant decision-maker (GM, plant or finance head, or a director) is reached, names a Thai-side or floor problem SAP does not cover, and is willing to discuss a complement that leaves the group SAP as it is.
- **Under a stated SAP and "no other ERP":** never a replacement; DigiWin is positioned as an extension beside SAP (Thai tax and statutory layer, BOI raw-material records only if the certificate carries raw-material privileges, floor lot records, PMC/stock), interfaces per §22, and only with the group IT's agreement. First step = the field owner learns whose SAP it is (Thai company code in the group SAP, a Thai instance, or B1) and who keeps Thai books; any route to the group runs through the field owner and her leader.
- **PDC / HPHT superhard floor themes (NOVA's, questions only, no figures):** trace a finished cutter (with its ultrasonic inspection record) back to the press run's synthesis parameters, the assembly and the diamond-powder and carbide-substrate lots; press consumables (anvils, pressure-medium blocks) life and replacement records; powder mixing error-proofing and WIP by press; press planning across mixing, assembly, synthesis, leaching and grinding so presses do not wait.
- Caution (Claude vetting): NOVA claimed group SAP templates support Thai VAT and withholding tax "very poorly" with "costly customisation", that SAP "usually cannot" handle BOI write-offs, that SAP's floor detail is "usually not enough" and that its standard modules cannot refine PMC (unverified competitor claims, never said); described a "mature DigiWin TH Local Tax system that integrates with SAP" (needs TH product confirmation); cited "API" audit standards (not evidenced); proposed using the parent's IPO background to reach group IT or finance with a "blueprint" deliverable not known to exist, asking the regional head to move the colleague's 私田 lead to 公田 or "協同攻堅", and a "Call High" to the two directors (not adopted: Peter does not take or work a colleague's account or call the customer's directors); named references 奔朗新材, 沃爾德, 歐科億, 華銳精密 and 保來得 (none in the TW case mirror or the Monk cards; not used). Verified adjacent cases in the mirror: 兆鑫精密 779 (2018, powder metallurgy, Smart ERP, formula cost with recycled material), 阡鴻實業 1021 (2018, powder-metallurgy sintered parts, Workflow ERP, stock accuracy 80 to 90), 心源工業 3096 (2022) and 3444 (2023) (cutting tools; lead time and obsolete-scrap results). The sweep's direct PDC and superhard hits in the mirror and the Monk cards were all string noise (PDCA, a Windows PDC server); the RAG was not searched (not installed), so whether DigiWin has a PDC case stays open. No control-workaround, pretext or recording advice was offered.

## §145 — ACP D whose only daily report is "待联系" (never contacted, 26 days; contact record from a data list naming a sole-signing director plus the HR recruiting contact, on a mail domain that does not exist) at a RAMPING four-factory Thai FURNITURE and household-goods export plant (registered 2023-01, ฿60.24M paid in, ฿1.11B PP&E, first sales year FY2024 ~฿170M at a ~฿109M loss, stock ~3.0× cost of sales, negative equity, ~฿1.58B interest-light current liabilities; self-described 1,600 staff, 200-plus SKUs, iron beds and racks to US retailers) that runs a customs FREE ZONE (เขตปลอดอากร) and is hiring a Free Zone clerk (e-Customs, Free Zone stock control, monthly/half-year/annual customs reports, "can use ERP") and an accountant ("ERP / SAP / Kingdee / Yonyou a plus"), 100 Hong Kong-held, self-described member of an UNRESOLVED "Huya Group"; lead in a colleague's 私田 (NOVA live 2026-10-25 · job 24b40a10 · THAIYA CORPORATION run · 南高-商機助手)
- **Grade (NOVA, this time):** never contacted but with live operating-pain hiring = **D (需求探詢) on a two-week clock**; if the field owner has no first real conversation within two weeks, **E**. This differs from §142 (contact data alone = E): NOVA lets live, specific hiring signals hold D briefly; without them §142 stands. C2 = a conversation with the plant's finance head or a director/project lead in which they acknowledge a Free Zone stock-reporting or group-ERP localisation gap that has no internal consensus yet.
- **Customs Free Zone ≠ BOI (reusable):** a Free Zone plant's records are customs stock cards (raw material, WIP, finished goods in and out, scrap, variances) reconciled against e-Customs import/export entries, with periodic reports for customs audit. NOVA says DigiWin TH has Free Zone stock-card reporting on Workflow GP / T100 and can match e-Customs entry data to ERP receipts and shipments. **Unverified: get DigiWin TH delivery's written confirmation** of (a) Free Zone report coverage, (b) whether it can run as a report layer beside a group ERP (Kingdee, Yonyou, SAP) or needs the full ERP, (c) Rayong consultants with Free Zone audit experience, before any customer claim.
- **Metal and flat-pack furniture floor themes (NOVA's, questions only, no figures):** how the physical count of raw material, WIP and finished goods stays in step with the Free Zone stock card and how count differences are declared; tube and sheet offcut recovery in cutting, stamping and bending and how the loss reaches product cost; whether pickling and powder coating are in-house or subcontracted and how WIP at the subcontractor is tracked; kit completeness for flat-pack export (carton, hardware pack, manual) so parts are not missed.
- **Group ERP unknown at a PRC group's Thai plant:** the field owner asks the plant's finance side directly and truthfully whether Thailand keeps the group's system or may evaluate a Thai-localised one; never through a recruiting or HR pretext.
- **Credit prudence (adopted):** at a negative-equity plant funded by group current liabilities, payment terms and any parent support are a normal commercial check at proposal time.
- Caution (Claude vetting): NOVA proposed opening through HR "以招聘協助與合規諮詢為由...側抄" IT facts (pretext; not adopted), scripted "我們在羅勇工業區協助許多中資企業解決 Free Zone 帳務無法與集團 SAP/金蝶/用友自動對齊" (unverified claim) and a "免費的 Free Zone 系統合規對照表" (deliverable not known to exist); called DigiWin TH's Free Zone reporting "成熟" and audit-compliant (unverified); urged the SDR to start within three days or "釋出公田或進行協作" lest 用友/金蝶 "抄走" it (account allocation is the field owners' and leaders' call; fear framing); named references 中策橡膠 Thailand, 建大 Kenda, 美克 Markor Vietnam, 振興家具 and 大發家具 Vietnam (none in the TW case mirror or the Monk cards, 2026-10-25; not used). No control-workaround, tax, recording or false-identity advice was adopted.

## §146 — ACP D whose only daily report is "泰国人听不懂英文，待后续官明协助联系" (the SDR's call reached a Thai-speaking line that could not converse in English; waiting for the Thai-speaking colleague; contact record "朱總" from a data list on the group's mail domain; 26 days quiet; amount 0) at a PROFITABLE Thai label-facestock COATING and slitting plant (registered 2019-01, FY2025 ~฿290M sales, ~฿40M net, stock ~171 days of cost of sales, own BOI-backed plant since 2024-03, two coating lines, more than half to the US per the MD in 2023) held 75 by a PRC label-film group in a BEIJING STOCK EXCHANGE IPO review (its 2020 ChiNext prospectus names the Thai company as a controlled grand-subsidiary) and 25 by two Singaporean individuals, one of them the English-speaking MD; any one of four directors signs; lead in a colleague's 私田 (NOVA live 2026-10-25 · job bf99b393 · HSF FILMS (THAILAND) run · 南高-商機助手)
- **Grade (NOVA):** no real conversation yet = **D (需求探詢), noted as a C2 candidate**; C2 only when a decision-holder (the MD, the group-side contact or group finance) confirms a Thai-plant pain (stock accuracy, cost close, audit data, BOI records) and agrees to a first exchange. A failed first call caused by language is a routing problem, not a customer signal: it neither lifts nor drops the grade by itself.
- **Language routing (adopted, truthful only):** where management works in English and the switchboard in Thai, the Thai-speaking colleague asks in Thai for the MD's office, identifies DigiWin truthfully, and the conversation continues in English; a Chinese-speaking SDR is the natural route only for a contact who is genuinely group-side.
- **Pre-IPO PRC parent, partly-owned Thai subsidiary (NOVA's reading, unverified):** the group wants subsidiary stock and books auditable and consistent for the sponsor and auditors; it usually prefers one group system but may allow a Thai-localised system feeding group consolidation when the group system lacks Thai service, Thai tax or BOI support. Treat as a hypothesis to test with the plant's finance side, never as a claim.
- **Coated-film and label-stock floor themes (NOVA's, questions only, no figures):** unit conversion from base film bought by weight to coated jumbo rolls and slit rolls sold by area and roll, with the losses between; trace-back from a slit roll number to the jumbo roll, base-film lot, coating batch and shift when a label-stock customer complains; how slitting knife plans are chosen to use the jumbo-roll width and how edge trim and seconds are costed; how jumbo rolls waiting to cure or to be slit are tracked as WIP so stock stays accurate.
- Caution (Claude vetting): NOVA scripted the SDR calling the group HQ as "鼎捷軟體（無錫總部的合作夥伴/在地專業 ERP 廠商）" to win a referral "以協助海外子公司 IPO 財務合規...為由" (a partnership not established; false-identity risk; not adopted), told Peter's side to fetch white papers from DigiWin TW/East China (not adopted for Peter), asserted stock pain "必然存在巨大痛點" and IPO "IPO 輔導經驗" as fact (unverified), and named references 萬洲化學 ACHEM, 炎洲 YEN SUN and 四維企業 (none in the TW case mirror or the Monk cards, 2026-10-25; not used). Verified adjacent cases in the mirror: 弘勝達科技 968 (2018; PET/OPP coating, metallizing, slitting; Workflow ERP; stock accuracy 100, close moved from the 25th to the 15th), 合富工業 1567 (2018; polyester film roll slitting, item numbers over ten thousand; Workflow ERP 2012, then group plants 2013), 宏櫂實業 603 (2018; TPU and hot-melt film; lot number per roll, label printing at receipt). No control-workaround, tax, recording or approval-limit advice was adopted.

## §147 — ACP D whose only daily report is "未接电话，待联系" (call not answered; contact from a data list on the SISTER's mail domain) at a PROFITABLE-but-break-even Thai plastic-injection and mould plant (~฿392M FY2025, gross profit equal to SG&A, net ~0.4, receivables ~98–131 days, near-zero stock on the books, Thai 52 / Chinese 48, one sole director who also signs for a same-address mould sister), live job ads asking for an unnamed "ERP" plus IATF 16949 / 8D; the sister's name is on DigiWin Thailand's own customer logo wall; the two companies sit in TWO colleagues' 私田 (NOVA live 2026-10-25 · job 419b036f · TOP PLASTIC run · 南高-商機助手)
- **Grade (NOVA):** an unanswered call is no conversation = **D (需求探詢)**, even with a logo-wall sister. C2 = a real conversation with the signatory or a named owner-side contact who states a process pain or an interest in managing the two companies together, without internal consensus yet; C1 = 立案三要項 (quantified must-do such as a customer IATF audit block or overhead that keeps overrunning, a named project contact, a committed evaluation or go-live date).
- **Installed-base check first (reusable):** when a sister appears on DigiWin's logo wall, the field owners' LEADER starts an internal check before any further call: CRM and DigiWin TH customer and contract records searched by company name, mail domain and contact names; contract number, product and version, MA status, and the original sales rep, consultant and customer-side implementation contact. A logo wall is marketing material, ACP's contract-record table may be empty, and 累計銷售金額 0 then proves nothing either way.
- **Two 私田 holders for one group:** the leader links the two ACP records as one group case; one SDR leads first contact (the one whose contact record reaches the operating plant), the other supplies account history; credit is agreed by the leader in advance (NOVA's 70/30 is an example, not a rule).
- **Conditional play:** if the group runs DigiWin, position a multi-company add-on plus mould and quality records (mould history, shot counts, maintenance; 8D and 4M change records for IATF audits) on the existing base; if it runs another ERP, it is a separate competitive case and needs its own evidence. First theme for an automotive moulder with overhead eating the gross margin: cost of poor quality, mould history and 8D, asked as questions; small phased scope (mould and quality first, shop floor later) with normal payment-term care.
- Caution (Claude vetting): NOVA opened with "提升模具修模效率與自動化排程" (no automatic-scheduling promise on Workflow; APS is T100 tier), framed an owner question on the customer's own rising SG&A (never quote filings at the customer), read the margin as "極大的無形浪費" (over-reading; questions only), and used an HQ-side "海外事業部（泰國分公司）調閱資料" route (read as DigiWin TH's own service records, asked by the field owners' leader). No control-workaround, tax, recording, false-identity or approval-limit advice was offered or adopted.

## §148 — ACP D whose only daily report is "7月10号参会客户，加上了微信，客户已有ERP。虽然有BOI核销痛点，但是客户不约" (event attendee on WeChat; says an ERP is already in place; names a BOI write-off pain; declines any meeting; contact = a data-list "陳董", not the registered director; amount 0; 33 days quiet) at a JUST-BUILT, pre-revenue Thai automotive interior and exterior OEM-parts plant (registered 2023-11, ฿300M registered, ~฿78.6M paid in, PP&E ~฿243.5M at the FY2025 close, zero stock and sales, ~฿278.8M interest-free current liabilities) on 20 rai in WHA Industrial Estate Rayong, 99.9 held by one Singapore holder, described by the estate developer as a Ningbo-headquartered maker (parent unresolved); lead in a colleague's 私田 (NOVA live 2026-10-25 · job 1ae5aa06 · FUCHENG TECHNOLOGY (THAILAND) run · 南高-商機助手)
- **Grade:** a customer-stated BOI pain with a refusal to meet, no project team, no amount and a month of silence = **D (需求探詢 / 待激活)**; saying the keyword does not lift the stage (行為序判定). Consistent with §83 (stated BOI pain at an HQ-controlled plant = C2 only while re-engagement is live; drop to D after ~15 days). Lift to C2: the customer agrees to even a short online or offline exchange, or shares the BOI certificate (privileges), the existing ERP brand or concrete pain detail. C1 = the 立案三要項.
- **Side-car play under "already have ERP":** never challenge the existing (probably group-chosen) ERP; offer the Thai BOI layer beside it: RMTS (formula, scrap rates, maximum quantities, release and write-off reports from actual receipts, issues and stock) plus eMT (machinery register tied to fixed assets). Interface wording per §22.
- **Three facts the SDR clarifies first (open questions on WeChat):** (A) does the BOI certificate carry raw-material duty exemption (if not, no RMTS need; machinery only); (B) was the ERP imposed by the group or chosen by the Thai plant; (C) is BOI filed by an in-house BOI officer (more open to a system) or an outside agent (may resist one).
- **Route:** the SDR's leader checks CRM and, at the leader's choice, asks DigiWin's office in the parent's city whether the parent is a DigiWin customer (in-family → Thai BOI layer on the same base, §83). The channel head does not drive this on a colleague's account.
- **Interior and exterior trim floor questions (new plant, questions only):** mould shot counts and maintenance during trial runs; regrind-to-virgin resin ratio control; tracing a paint defect to rack, process settings and paint lot; colour-change sequencing on the paint line; packing and labels matching each car maker's barcode format.
- Caution (Claude vetting): NOVA's scripts used fear framing (customs holds on the first duty-free imports, "大筆資金補稅", "高額滯港費", "病急亂投醫"); claimed "我們服務過許多羅勇當地的汽車零件廠" (unverified) and that "多數中資系統在泰國無法自動產出 RMTS 標準申報格式" (unverified competitor claim); proposed sending a "BOI 申報地雷自檢表" or a "3 大避坑指南" PDF "以「資料贈送」為名，降低防備" (deliverables not known to exist); guessed the parent's Chinese name and the director's characters; had the parent-city AM "Call High" the parent CIO and promised the lead would become "集團協同 A/B 級"; named BYD and Great Wall in a floor question with no link to the company; estimated mass production "2026" without evidence. None adopted. No control-workaround, tax, recording or false-identity advice was adopted. Separately, the internal Thailand_BOI_Sales_Playbook's "申請時將損耗率拉滿 = 合法安全墊" and "兩套帳" wording are not to be used with customers (gaming wastage applications is a tax-obligation workaround; describe the method only as mapping item codes to BOI material groups in one set of books).

## §149 — ACP D whose only daily report says a Chinese-national INTERPRETER ("翻译，中国人小叶") took the call, would not give the Chinese management contact and said "有需求再联系" (contact record from a data list naming the CEO, who is one of three sole-signing DBD directors; amount 0; 31 days quiet) at a RAMPING Thai benchtop power-tool plant (miter and table saws; registered 2023-02, ฿593.26M capital, about ฿463M paid in, first full sales year ~฿221M at ~3.9 gross margin, stock ~182 days of cost of sales, ~฿389M PP&E, US-bound bills of lading) held 100 by three Singapore-nationality holders and self-described as the plant of a Jinhua (Zhejiang) private power-tool OEM/ODM; the plant's own job ads hire a BOI officer for duty refunds, release of raw materials and machinery and extension of their import periods, and a Chinese-speaking buyer; lead in a colleague's 私田 (NOVA live 2026-10-25 · job f20a6c4d · HUA FENG TOOLS (THAILAND) run · 南高-商機助手)
- **Grade:** a gatekeeper who closes the door, no need, no decision-holder in conversation = **E (公田 / 長期經營)**; the SDR's D is recorded, not changed (consistent with §104 and §142). Back to D = a real conversation with a working-level owner (the new BOI officer, purchasing or finance) who admits the BOI write-off or stock work is heavy, or that Thai and parent data do not reconcile. C2 = a director or plant head (here the CEO, a DBD signatory) engages on the ramp's cost or stock and agrees to look at a system. C1 = the 立案三要項.
- **Interpreter gatekeeper (adopted, truthful only):** speak Chinese to a Chinese-national interpreter; make her the beneficiary, not the obstacle: offer something genuinely useful she can pass upward (e.g. a short note on what a new BOI officer handles in the first year), identify DigiWin Thailand truthfully, and never claim "we are not selling". Only assets that really exist may be offered.
- **Route:** the field owner's leader may ask DigiWin China's office in the parent's city (1) is the parent a DigiWin customer and on what system, (2) who decides overseas-plant systems (HQ IT template or the Thai CEO), (3) what the parent struggles with in controlling the overseas plant (item codes, stock visibility, consolidation). The channel head never calls DigiWin China or the customer himself on a colleague's account.
- **First cut (NOVA):** the Thai BOI raw-material layer (RMTS: formula, release, write-off, import-period extension) plus stock control, not a full Thai ERP first; reason: the plant's own BOI-officer hire and a large stock on a thin margin. Claude's condition: confirm first that the BOI certificate carries raw-material duty exemption (§148 fact A); without it the play is machinery records and stock only.
- **Power-tool OEM/ODM assembly floor questions (questions only, no figures):** how brand- and voltage-specific parts (motors, blades, cords, labels, manuals) are kept from being mixed up at changeovers between brands; how key safety parts (motor, switch, battery pack where cordless) are traced to a serial or lot when a US or EU customer asks; whether line-side WIP and the books agree at count; how duty-free BOI material is told apart from locally bought material on the floor.
- Caution (Claude vetting): NOVA scripted the SDR saying "我們不是來推銷系統的" (untrue) and offering a 《BOI 合規紅線與常見稽核避坑指南》 white paper "免費" to obtain WeChat or the boss's contact (no such asset verified; a pretext; not adopted); argued the must-do with fear framing ("被泰國海關課稅或補稅，4% 的利潤會瞬間歸零，甚至面臨巨額罰款", "避免海關實地查核時發現") (not adopted); proposed a Call High topic built on the plant's filing figures (4 per cent margin, 182 days) (never quote filings at the customer, §43); wrote the CEO's name in guessed Chinese characters (辛華軍; characters not published); named 正航 and Express as likely low-price interceptors (unverified). It was not told that the ACP contact record names the CEO himself. No control-workaround, approval-limit, recording or direct-contact advice was adopted.

## §150 — ACP D whose only daily report is "客户泰国人未转接，待再联系" (a Thai staff member answered and did not put the call through; no one spoken to; contact record from a data list naming a 法人代表 on the GROUP's mail domain, not the Thai director; amount 0; 31 days quiet) at a RAMPING Thai automotive engine-air and cabin-filter plant in WHA Industrial Estate Rayong (registered 2023-10, ฿330M fully paid, PP&E ~฿381M, first sales year FY2025 ~฿61M at ~32 gross margin and a ~฿35M loss, receivables ~411 days, interest-light group liabilities, US-bound bills of lading), 99.45 Chinese-nationality capital + 0.55 Canadian, sole Chinese director, trading as the Shanghai brand QAP (6,000-plus part numbers, group IATF 16949 self-described, Thai OE target), live job ads for a Japanese-trained Lean specialist, three production supervisors and a QC officer; no system named anywhere; lead in a colleague's 私田 (NOVA live 2026-10-25 · job 2883e4c7 · QAP FILTER (THAILAND) run · 南高-商機助手)
- **Grade (NOVA):** an untransferred call is no conversation = **E (長期經營)**, the same reading as §149 (gatekeeper closes the door); §146/§147 held D for a language mix-up or an unanswered call, so record NOVA's grade and leave the ACP grade to the field owner. C2 = a real conversation with a pain-bearing or decision-influencing person (the director, plant head or finance head) who admits a part-number, stock or traceability bottleneck while internal consensus is still missing.
- **Switchboard routing (adopted, truthful only):** ask by NAME for the director's office, or by role for the Chinese-speaking finance or plant head; identify DigiWin Thailand truthfully; use a Thai-speaking colleague when the switchboard needs Thai. A routing failure is not a customer signal.
- **First cut for a ramping high-SKU auto-filter plant aiming at OE (NOVA, hypothesis):** ERP core (purchasing, stock, production BOM, receivables and payables) + lot traceability + barcode labels; reason: OE audits ask for forward and backward traceability and labelled shipments. A Lean/TPS hire changes the framing (ERP as the data base for Lean: standard times, reported output, material flow), not the phase-1 scope; worker- and line-level data stays eMES Phase 2 (Claude's condition, per the pain-product table).
- **Parent check:** leader-to-leader inside DigiWin (the SDR's leader with the office in the parent's city) to learn whether the parent is a DigiWin customer and who decides overseas systems; the channel head never calls DigiWin China or the customer on a colleague's account.
- Caution (Claude vetting): NOVA told the SDR "切忌透露「我是來賣系統的」" (concealment; not adopted), suggested LinkedIn messaging to the plant's Chinese managers (run rule: no LinkedIn outreach) and calling the HR number on the job ads to ask whether new hires need "鼎捷系統" experience while offering training support (recruiting pretext; not adopted); scripted "我們輔導過許多華東濾清器大廠落地泰國" (unverified); quoted "人工整理追溯報告需 3 至 5 天 / 系統化後 5 分鐘" and "單次損失可能高達數十萬泰銖" (unsourced, fear framing); offered an "IATF 16949 追溯白皮書" (not known to exist) and a congratulation on the capital increase (quoting filings at the customer, §43); named 蚌埠昊風過濾器 and 浙江金威過濾器 as DigiWin filter cases (neither in the TW case mirror or the Monk cards, 2026-10-25; not used). Verified adjacent cases: 記銘工業 626 (2018; auto parts; Workflow ERP + PDA barcode; average lead time 26 → 15 days), 三卯鍛壓 3254 (2022; auto forgings; sMES), 凡事康流體 1117 (2018; filter cartridges; Workflow ERP + EasyFlow). No control-workaround, tax, approval-limit, recording or false-identity advice was adopted.

## §151 — ACP D whose only daily report is "总经理助理 Sylvia，告知有资料可发" (the GM's ASSISTANT agreed to receive material by email on the PARENT's mail domain; the ACP contact record is a data-list MISMATCH pointing at a same-sounding Bangkok distributor), at a RAMPING, heavily loss-making Thai construction-machinery parts plant (excavator booms, arms, pins; FY2025 sales ~฿395M ×9.6, net loss ~฿116M, SG&A ~42 of sales, receivables ~322 days, ~฿1B interest-free group liabilities, ฿300M fully paid, Hong Kong 90 / China 10) of a PRIVATE Hangzhou group (Caterpillar supplier-quality awards, IATF 16949) whose OWN website shows a self-built AI scheduling and drawing-inspection platform with "数据源 QAD" for all plants; Thai plant hiring a BOI Specialist (cut-stock, EMT / e-Tracking / e-Invoice) and an order controller with ERP skills and Thai tax invoices; lead in a colleague's 私田 (NOVA live 2026-10-25 · job 2529f8a5 · UNITED MECHANICAL TECHNOLOGY (THAILAND) run · 南高-商機助手)
- **Stage:** an assistant who will receive material, no need, no decision-maker, no date = **E (公田/長期經營)**, below the SDR's D. Lift facts: (1) an evidenced Thai-side pain the plant cannot handle by hand (BOI cut-stock, Thai tax documents, customer-audit traceability); (2) head office's IT policy for the Thai plant (QAD mandated, or local add-ons allowed); (3) the assistant becomes a supporter who refers upward to the plant head or group IT.
- **Group on a global ERP (QAD) plus its OWN AI layer:** never challenge the core; position DigiWin TH as the Thai compliance and traceability complement (two-tier): BOI raw-material reports and Thai tax documents beside the group ERP, and light floor data collection for heat-treatment / NDT / weld records only where the group's platform does not reach Rayong. Interface wording per §22 (agreed formats and upload files; no "real-time" or "seamless" promises). A self-built AI team = 自建情結: sell the standard data base under their platform, never a rival scheduler.
- **Material to the assistant:** only real, verifiable collateral (a published case of a Thai metal-parts plant on DigiWin ERP with Thai tax, a BOI capability note that actually exists) plus ONE neutral question: are BOI cut-stock and Thai tax documents produced inside the group ERP or alongside it? Never open with the plant's filing figures or its customers' names.
- **Loss-making, group-funded plant:** every spend is a head-office decision; the route to the parent (is it a DigiWin China account, what does it run, who decides overseas systems) is the field owner's leader's call inside DigiWin.
- Caution (Claude vetting): NOVA called QAD's Thai and BOI localisation "極弱且客製費昂貴" (unverified competitor claim); named two collateral titles that are not known to exist; drafted an opener congratulating the plant on its filed revenue and naming Caterpillar audits, aimed to "引發危機感" (quoting filings, fear framing); told the channel head to engage DigiWin China's Hangzhou office and "Call High" the parent himself (not adopted: Peter does not contact DigiWin China or work a colleague's account); framed value as "少繳稅" and "避免丟單" (reworded as correct BOI records and audit-ready traceability); gave the signatory a "董事長" title and guessed a sister entity name (unverified); promised HQ "即時掌握" through an API (no real-time promise). Adopted: grade E with lift facts, the complement position, the neutral question, the internal route, three floor questions (heat-treatment batch records, outsourced plating reconciliation, CMM / NDT data and SPC). No control-workaround, recording or false-identity advice was adopted.

## §152 — ACP D whose only daily report is BLANK (date and grade only) and whose only contact record is a data-list name on a JOB-BOARD APPLICANT RELAY mailbox (trustmail.jobthai.com = the recruiting desk), at a PROFITABLE, ramping Thai plant of an UNRESOLVED PRC private owner (registered 2023-06, ฿60M, ~฿57M paid in, two Chinese holders, two Chinese directors either of whom signs alone; air-conditioner accessories from injection moulding, steel-wire welding and packaging at WHA ESIE 3, the estate of Haier's new AC plant, link unverified; sales ~฿97M → ~฿208M while gross margin fell from about 27 to about 11 and stock grew faster than sales; interest-free owner funding; full-rate tax, no BOI found; hiring "many" for an expansion); lead in a colleague's 私田 (NOVA live 2026-10-25 · job c5108eea · XINGYE RUBBER AND PLASTIC ELECTRONIC TECHNOLOGY run · 南高-商機助手)
- **Grade:** blank report + recruiting-relay contact + amount 0 = **E (公田/長期經營)**, below the SDR's D (same as §142). D = a working-level person in the plant (warehouse, production control, finance) who feels a pain (knows stock is too high or margin fell but not why); C2 = a power person (a director or the plant head) shows intent to solve it without internal consensus yet; C1 = the 立案三要項.
- **A job-board relay mailbox on an ACP contact record is the HR desk, not a project contact (reusable):** record it as the gatekeeper; never route a sales approach through the recruiting channel under a pretext (§145). The field owner calls the plant, says plainly she is DigiWin TH, and asks who looks after finance and the plant.
- **First cut when margin halves while volume doubles and the system is unknown:** ERP first (actual cost per work order, margin per part family and customer, stock by lot including regrind and wire offcuts, Thai tax), floor eMES later; consistent with §1, §86. Parent-system branches are hypotheses until the parent is known: DigiWin China = group template; Kingdee / Yonyou = Thai localisation and local service; SAP = two-tier with §22 interface wording.
- **Floor questions for injection + wire-welding + packaging plants (questions only):** how regrind and wire offcuts are measured and returned to use against issues; how WIP between moulding, welding and packing is counted and moved; how mould and welding-fixture life and maintenance are recorded and reach the plan; whether margin per product line explains the drop (material, scrap or one product).
- NOVA's open questions worth asking the customer: a parent listing plan (audit-grade systems) and a BOI application plan (bonded raw-material records).
- Caution (Claude vetting): NOVA scripted the SDR calling the recruiting contact "以『協助落地』為由" to "繞過門神" and collect the plant head's WeChat / LINE / phone (pretext through HR; not adopted), claimed "同園區的客戶案例" (no verified same-estate case; not usable), asserted "存貨積壓、生產報廢黑洞" and that full-tax filing without BOI is "極為繁重" (claims on assumptions), said Kingdee / Yonyou have no direct team or Thai tax / BOI solution in Thailand (unverified; contradicts §60), called SAP unaffordable at ฿60M capital (unsourced) and offered an API return to HQ as a promise (§22). Adopted: grade E with lift facts, ERP first, the three branches, four floor questions, the two open questions, Peter behind the field owner. No control-workaround, tax, recording or false-identity advice was adopted.

## §153 — ACP D whose only daily report is "待联系" (never contacted, 31 days; contact record a data-list name on the PARENT's mail domain, not a director; amount 0) at a RAMPING Thai WOOD-and-PAPER industrial-packaging plant (registered 2023-08, ฿180M fully paid, 23-plus rai in Rojana Nong Yai, PP&E ~฿359M, FY2025 sales ~฿127M at ~12 gross margin and a small loss, stock ~238 days of cost of sales, receivables ~261 days, ~฿458M interest-free non-current group liabilities) held Hong Kong 98 / Malaysia 2 and named in its SSE-LISTED parent's 2025 annual report as a wholly owned producing subsidiary, where the parent's own careers page (2026-01-27) names the group stack "U9 ERP、致遠OA、MES、物流運輸系統" and the annual report commits to rolling out ERP and MES for 業財一體化; lead in a colleague's 私田 (NOVA live 2026-10-25 · job 4c06cc4c · XINTONGLIAN PACKING (THAILAND) run · 南高-商機助手)
- **Grade:** never contacted = **E (公田 / 長期經營)** even with live Thai hiring signals and a known group ERP; NOVA held §142 here rather than §145's two-week D. D = a real conversation with the Thai plant (finance, plant head, warehouse or QA lead) in which they recognise an operating or Thai-tax problem; C2 = a power person (plant head or the HQ decision-maker) wants it solved and internal consensus is not yet there.
- **Positioning beside a group Yonyou U9 (reusable):** the wholly owned Thai plant's core ERP follows HQ policy; never pitch to replace U9. The only openings are the Thai statutory layer (Thai tax documents and books) and the plant floor (barcode stock receipts and issues, cutting records, heat-treatment batch records), with data passed to U9 through a standard interface per §22 (no seamless-sync promise; the U9-side work is the customer's with its vendor). Biggest risk, in NOVA's words: HQ pushes U9 end to end and the Thai plant has no say.
- **Wood-crate and carton floor questions (questions only, never quote the filing at the customer, §43):** how a new customer specification is costed, including lumber and plywood cutting yield, and how offcuts are controlled and reused; how ISPM 15 heat-treatment batch records (temperature and time) tie to stock and to each shipment's phytosanitary trail; what drives today's stock and collection cycle in the start-up phase (lumber drying and bulk buying, or customer terms); how the HQ system is being rolled out to Thai staff and what gets in the way of recording receipts and issues in real time.
- **Route:** the field owner contacts the Thai plant herself with truthful identification; any HQ-level approach through DigiWin China is for the field owners' leader to decide. Peter does not work a colleague's 私田 account and does not contact DigiWin China.
- Caution (Claude vetting): NOVA said a PRC-brand ERP's Thai tax localisation is "通常是弱項" (unverified; contradicts §60 and §152), scripted "我們在泰國協助過許多陸資製造業落地" (unverified claim), suggested "請上海鼎捷大客戶部...接觸上海新通聯總部資訊總監" (DigiWin China route, left to the field owners' leader), proposed quoting the 238-day stock figure and "釋放近 3,600 萬泰銖" to persuade management (filing figures at the customer; kept internal as a question), addressed the owner as 顧董 (identity of the Thai director is inferred, not proven), claimed rich TW/China wood-carrier and pallet cases (none in the TW mirror or the Monk cards, 2026-10-25), and asked whether Peter could co-own or take over the 私田 (allocation is the field owners' and leaders' call). Adopted: grade E with lift conditions, the U9-complement positioning, the four floor questions (reworded), the risk note. No control-workaround, tax, recording or false-identity advice was adopted.

## §154 — ACP D whose only daily report is "中国财务不在，未转接，待再联系" (the SDR asked for the Chinese finance person, who was out; call not put through; 31 days quiet; amount 0) and whose only contact record is a data-list name on a STAFFING-AGENCY corporate mailbox (Adecco), at a MICRO year-one Thai electronic-components company (registered 2023-11 with a Bangkok number, head office now Bo Win, Chonburi; ฿1M → ฿4M → ฿10M registered, about ฿7M paid in; Thai 51 / Chinese 49; one Chinese sole director; first sales year FY2025 ~฿7.07M at ~38 gross margin, ~฿0.34M profit, ZERO year-end stock, receivables ~2.5 days, ~฿9.78M of machines bought the same year, ~฿2.54M interest-free current liabilities; no website, job post, press or parent anywhere) (NOVA live 2026-10-25 · job 36b656d3 · ZHONGLIAN XINXI (THAILAND) run · 南高-商機助手)
- **Grade:** an untransferred call plus a recruiter-mailbox contact = **E (公田 / 長期經營)**, below the SDR's D (same as §150, §152). C2 = contact with the sole director or the Chinese finance head who agrees to meet and admits a material-control or Thai-tax management concern in the new plant.
- **Zero year-end stock + fresh capex + small sales (reusable reading):** two hypotheses, never one: (a) processing on customer- or parent-supplied material (the margin is a processing-fee margin), (b) trial-scale output with equipment just installed. First-call questions (truthful, no filing figures quoted): is the material supplied by the customer or the owner, or bought in Thailand; does the owner want Thailand to run its own Thai-tax system or only report production and work-order progress to someone else.
- **Size and shape:** a ~฿7M shop gets no presales and never a big ERP. If a need is stated, the only first cut is a light cloud purchase-sales-stock scope that also keeps customer-supplied material (receipts, issues, scrap, returns) and work-order progress; consistent with §96, §130, §131. Watch-list, not pursuit.
- **Re-entry triggers:** job-board postings (JobThai / JobsDB) for a production controller (PMC), accountant or plant manager; signs that the plant is operating at volume; sales growth toward the ฿50M line; a parent identified as a DigiWin account. Risk note: decision rights may sit wholly with an owner outside Thailand if one exists (hypothesis while no parent is evidenced).
- **A staffing-agency mailbox on an ACP contact is a recruiting channel, not a project contact (reusable, extends §152):** the field owner calls the company's own line again, identifies DigiWin Thailand plainly, asks for the director by name or the Chinese finance head by role; never routes a sales message through the agency.
- Caution (Claude vetting): NOVA opened with "恭喜貴司在 Bo Win 投資了近千萬的生產設備" (filing figures at the customer, §43), framed the material question around BOI and bonded accounts (no BOI evidenced; the 20 point tax rate argues against a holiday), scripted "在 Bo Win 工業區服務了非常多中資電子模具廠" and "豐富的電子與汽車零件同行導入經驗" (unverified), offered a 《中資企業在泰落地稅務與生管規劃手冊》 / 《中資建廠合規指南》 (no such asset verified; used as a door-opener = pretext), proposed a cold door-knock that quotes the filing, LinkedIn and Chinese-chamber WeChat outreach to the director (run rule: no LinkedIn outreach) introducing the SDR as "鼎捷泰國的 Nova" (false identity), and asking the Adecco recruiter to forward the guide (routing through the recruiting channel, §152); it quantified "每停工一天...數萬泰銖" and "稅務罰款風險" (fear framing, unsourced), speculated that the owner uses the company "用來避稅" (loaded, unfounded), guessed a "王總" and used the Taiwanese "三欄式發票" concept. None adopted. Adopted: grade E with the lift behaviour, the two-hypothesis reading, the two first-call questions (reworded), the light scope, the watch-list and job-board triggers, the risk note. No control-workaround, tax, audit, approval-limit or recording advice was adopted.

## §155 — ACP D whose only daily report is "待再联系" (no conversation recorded; the only contact is the plant's JOB-BOARD recruiting contact on a personal mailbox; amount 0; 31 days quiet) at a LARGE, very profitable, fast-ramping Thai BOI EXPORT plant (registered 2023-05, ฿163M, Singapore holder 99.5 / Chinese 0.5, two Chinese sole-signing directors; FY2025 ~฿1.38B, ×1.96, gross ~32, net ~22, zero income tax, stock ×15 to ~182 days of cost, receivables ~217 days) making automotive electric fuel pumps and injectors for US AFTERMARKET importers, self-described base of a Jiaxing fuel-pump group (5,000-plus part numbers, provincial digital-workshop and industrial-internet awards, ERP brand not public), with a probable sister plant company in Rayong (shared director, same holder split, pre-revenue); no system named anywhere; lead in a colleague's 私田 (NOVA live 2026-10-25 · job 16350d2a · JOINHANDS INJECTION SYSTEM (THAILAND) run · 南高-商機助手)
- **Grade (NOVA):** a single "to be contacted again" with only a recruiting contact = **E (公田 / 長期經營)**, whatever the plant's size; D needs a need-probing conversation. C2 = direct contact with the GM, finance head or plant/PMC head who confirms an operating pain (BOI material records, multi-SKU stock accuracy, data gap with HQ) and shows interest while no budget or timetable consensus exists. Size and filing strain make it an OSINT C2-candidate, not a pipeline C2.
- **Parent check first (adopted):** the SDR's leader asks, leader to leader inside DigiWin, the DigiWin China office covering the parent's city whether the parent is an account, which system and version, and the parent's policy for overseas plants (mandatory rollout, plant decides, or HQ-held budget). NOVA's internal unit names (海外事業部, 集團大客戶部, 華東大區) are its own and unverified.
- **Architecture (adopted as two scenarios):** A, parent on DigiWin (T100 / TOPGP): Thai site on the parent platform plus DigiWin TH's Thai tax and BOI localisation, local consulting, Thai-language training and support, then WMS/MES; B, parent on another system: two-tier, a Thai ERP with Thai tax and BOI and a standard interface to the parent (standard APIs plus per-project mapping only, §22).
- **First cut for a many-SKU aftermarket export plant (hypothesis):** BOI material and machinery records reconciled against production and export (only once the certificate and privileges are confirmed) plus stock and lot control by part number; first-call question on how receipts, issues and finished stock for thousands of part numbers are recorded, never asserting buyer requirements.
- Caution (Claude vetting): NOVA scripted unverified claims ("專門協助中資汽車零部件企業落地", "正在與幾家 WHA 工業區的中資零部件企業合作"), a BOI/tax "white paper" and "建廠合規資料" door-opener (assets not known to exist: pretext), an HR-desk route with "罰款風險" fear framing, unsourced "被海關課稅" and "巨大的追溯與賠償風險", and a "反向滲透 / 跳過泰國當地的行政與 HR" route harvesting the plant head's WeChat or phone through HQ. None adopted except, in modified form, an open introduction requested by the account owner if the parent is a customer. No control-workaround, tax, audit, approval-limit, recording or false-identity advice was adopted.

## §156 — ACP D whose daily reports are an ORGANISATION MAP without a need ("客户运营总监 赵刚 / 内务：李 / 财务：耿經理 (earlier: 大陆财务定期出差过来) / 泰国2个本地财务 / 财务报税流程外包"; no contact record linked; amount 0; 26 days quiet) at a YEAR-ONE, loss-making Thai BATCH-CHEMICAL plant (cobalt-salt rubber adhesion promoters for global tyre makers; registered 2020 under another name, renamed 2024; ~฿519M paid in, ฿619M registered, PP&E ~฿488M; first manufacturing year FY2025 ~฿70M at ~3.3 gross margin, ~฿30M loss, stock ~309 days of cost; Singapore 89 / Chinese 11; one Chinese sole signatory) of a Jiangyin PRIVATE parent (12 lines, 15,000 t/yr across Jiangyin and Rayong, ERP brand not public); lead in a colleague's 私田 (NOVA live 2026-10-25 · job e0b9e6f0 · SANLIANG (THAILAND) run · 南高-商機助手)
- **Grade (NOVA):** an SDR conversation that yields the customer's roles and finance set-up but no pain = **D (需求探詢)**, not E: key windows are known and reachable. Contrast §150, §154, §155 (no one spoken to, or only a recruiter) = E. C2 = the operations director or finance manager admits an operating pain (lot trace, stock, cost, Thai books) and agrees to look at a system.
- **First cut for a ramping batch-chemical supplier to audit-heavy customers (adopted as hypothesis):** lead with lot and CoA traceability plus shelf life (what the customer's audits ask of the plant), not with Thai finance and tax: where tax filing is outsourced and HQ finance flies in, a finance-first pitch reads as "the current set-up is fine". Thai finance comes second, through the finance manager, framed as HQ's plan for the Thai books. BOI only after a certificate is confirmed.
- **Route:** operations director first (resident, owns delivery, quality audits and stock); the SDR's leader asks the DigiWin China office covering the parent's city whether the parent is an account, what it runs and who decides overseas-plant systems; no heavy free presales until the decision chain (Thai plant vs HQ owner) is known.
- **Tier:** NOVA leaned T100 (capital, group, batch process) with a phased start (purchasing, sales, stock and quality trace before finance and production); its claim that Workflow/iGP cannot carry batch processes or multiple units of measure was not verified and was not adopted; the tier call stays with /select-t100-vs-workflow.
- Caution (Claude vetting): NOVA scripted an unverified claim ("服務過很多羅勇府的汽車與橡膠零部件配套廠"), an unsourced fear line ("一旦料帳不符，重則面臨補稅重罰") presuming a BOI certificate, and "強勢腹地 / 對抗友商的王牌" boasts. None adopted; the first-call questions were reworded to plain, truthful asks. No control-workaround, tax, audit, approval-limit, recording or false-identity advice was adopted.

## §157 — ACP D whose only daily report is "待联系" (never contacted, 31 days; one data-list contact on the PARENT's Taiwan mail domain, role unknown) at a PRE-PRODUCTION Thai BONDED e-waste / precious-metal REFINERY (registered 2024-03, ฿300M registered, ~฿270M paid in by FY2025, PP&E ~฿144M land-and-build, pre-revenue; production planned ~June 2027, machinery import by end-2026) of a Taiwan OTC-listed, family-run recycler (TPEx 8390) that holds it through a Samoa vehicle (56, rising to 78 by a ฿300M subscription announced 2026-09-21), whose GM is the Thai sole director and signatory and whose own IT department is two people; parent ERP brand not public; the GM sits on the board of a company that is a 2018 DigiWin TW case; ACP's Chinese name was the parent's 40% associate, not the plant; lead in a colleague's 私田 (NOVA live 2026-10-25 · job c3d886fd · JYD PREMIUM MATERIALS TECHNOLOGY (THAILAND) run · 南高-商機助手)
- **Grade:** never contacted = **E (公田/長期經營)** even with a dated greenfield start and fresh parent capital (same as §142/§153; a capital raise and a production date do not replace a conversation). C2 = contact with the decision chain (parent GM / IT or the Thai build team) plus a stated wish to evaluate a system for the Thai plant.
- **First cut for a bonded recycling/refining greenfield (reusable):** Thai tax plus BOI/bonded material and raw-material records (with the build-phase machinery register) first, because they are hard compliance gates from the first machinery import to the first bonded receipt. Do NOT open with lot weighing, sampling and assay-based settlement: it is the refinery's core and the most complex fit question, and raising it before the plant's flows exist (and with a two-person HQ IT team) lifts the evaluation bar. Tier hypothesis: Workflow/iGP (NOVA), the call stays with /select-t100-vs-workflow.
- **Sign-by date:** with production ~June 2027 NOVA put the latest signing at 2026 Q3, no later than October 2026 (consistent with §17's ~9-month back-calc). When the research date is already at that limit, say so: a choice may be in progress without DigiWin.
- **Board-seat link to a DigiWin case company:** use it only as a lead for the field owners' leader (ask DigiWin TW whether the parent itself is an account and who decides overseas-plant systems); never ask the case company's people about the prospect's internal matters; never present the case company's 2018 system as current, and naming it to the prospect needs Peter's OK.
- Caution (Claude vetting): NOVA argued Workflow fit from the case company "currently" using Workflow (2018 page; case-currency rule), scripted "我們過去在台灣與海外協助過許多指標性的循環經濟與金屬回收企業（例如中台資源）" (unverified, names a customer), offered a 「泰國建廠 IT 規劃與 BOI 合規實務白皮書」 (deliverable not known to exist), misdated machinery arrival ("明年底" instead of end-2026), used "嚴格的法規限制" framing, and targeted the parent's investor-relations spokesperson for a sales call. None adopted. No control-workaround, tax, audit, approval-limit, recording or false-identity advice was offered or adopted.
