## §158 — ACP D whose only daily report is "日常联络" (content-free; contact = a data-list 高級專員, not a director, whose mailbox domain is PARKED FOR SALE) at a YEAR-ONE, 100 Taiwanese-held Bangkok IMPORTER-WHOLESALER of injection machines, moulds and resin (TSIC 46593; registered 2024-06; ฿5M → ฿200M on 2025-03-20, fully paid; FY2025 ฿1.01M revenue with NO cost of sales and NO stock, ฿57.9M new PP&E, ~฿152M other current assets, no bank debt; three Chinese-name directors, any one signs; brand and parent not in any registry or filing); lead in a colleague's 私田 (NOVA live 2026-10-25 · job 1a51ea8e · CHANG YU (THAILAND) run · 南高-商機助手)
- **Grade:** content-free contact note + unreachable contact = **E (公田 / 長期經營)**, below the SDR's D (same behaviour-sequence rule as §87/§111/§122/§123/§130). C2 = a confirmed real contact and role, a pain the customer states (Thai tax, import costing, after-sales dispatch) and agreement to meet DigiWin Thailand.
- **Reading the capital (Claude, from the BOI OSOS FAQ, info as of July 2015):** a foreign-owned trader paying in exactly ฿100M (wholesale) or ฿200M (wholesale plus retail) is most likely meeting the Foreign Business Act List 3 no-licence threshold; read it as a legal entry ticket, never as deal size. NOVA agreed.
- **Revenue with zero cost of sales and zero stock at an importer:** fees (commission on machines the principal ships direct, service, or rental of machines held as fixed assets), not resale; ask whether the principal ships direct or the Thai company will hold stock and install.
- **Minimum scope if a need is ever stated (NOVA, adopted as hypothesis):** Thai VAT/WHT localisation, import landed cost and multi-currency purchasing, stock and sales by machine serial number with warranty dates. No presales before a diagnosed need.
- **Dealer service/dispatch package — CONFLICTING NOVA ANSWERS:** §53 (2026-10-24) said DigiWin TH has NO Thai standard package for the equipment-agent service side; this consult said "Workflow ERP + FSM 行動派工 standard package" exists. Neither may be repeated to a customer until TH delivery confirms.
- **Sales target vs channel partner:** an injection-machine/mould/resin importer sells into the same moulding plants DigiWin TH sells to. First real call separates the two: which brands it carries, whether it holds stock and runs a service team in Thailand, what the Taiwan head office runs. Channel talk only through the field owner and /research-partner, once the brand is known.
- **Watch-list triggers:** website or FB page live; JobThai/JobsDB postings for sales, service engineers or an accountant; a Taiwanese machine brand launched in Thailand under the company; registry changes (directors, branch or warehouse); next filing showing cost of sales and stock.
- Caution (Claude vetting): NOVA scripted the caller as "鼎捷泰國的 Nova" (false identity) with a guessed "張經理", opened on the capital raise (filing figures at the customer, §43), claimed "非常多塑膠射出廠客戶" (unverified), offered to bring DigiWin customers to the company's showroom (a referral promise used as a door-opener), suggested re-confirming the contact via LinkedIn (not allowed) and pitching DigiWin's "FBA compliance expertise" (not a DigiWin service). None adopted. No control-workaround, tax, audit, approval-limit, recording or false-identity advice was adopted.

## §159 — ACP D whose two daily reports both read only "日常联络" (content-free; three data-list / event-list / DBD-crawler contacts with no titles, none shown to be the director, one on the probable group's mail domain; amount 0; 25 days quiet) at a BUILT-and-EQUIPPED, PRE-REVENUE Thai plastic-HOUSEWARES injection plant (registered 2024-08, ฿100M registered, ฿76.25M paid in, PP&E ~฿241M, zero sales and stock at the FY2025 close, ~฿274M interest-free long-term liabilities; Singapore 55 / China 45, sole director signs alone), very likely the Thai plant of a Taizhou housewares exporter (three China plants, US big-box retail customers, hit by the April 2025 US tariffs) that owns the brand trademark; legal parent unproven; hiring an interpreter for Chinese managers; lead in a colleague's 私田 (NOVA live 2026-10-25 · job 695bb3f5 · SEEAYA HOUSEWARE (THAILAND) run · 南高-商機助手)
- **Grade:** content-free contact notes = **E (公田 / 長期經營)**, below the SDR's D, even for a funded plant about to start (same behaviour-sequence rule as §87/§122/§123/§158). C2 lift facts: reach the director, a build lead or the finance head; the customer names a start-up problem (Thai tax, consolidation with the China HQ, new-plant standards); the customer agrees to an initial meeting.
- **Phase 1 for a just-built housewares injection plant:** finance with Thai localisation, fixed assets from the build, purchasing, sales and stock, basic production (BOM, work orders, material issue); no MES or APS in phase 1; BOI accounting only if a certificate exists. Consistent with §54/§107/§124.
- **Housewares-injection question themes (questions, never claims or figures):** colour and mould changeovers; item numbering by size, colour and lid; US big-box vendor compliance (carton/pallet labels, shipping documents, chargebacks for errors); regrind (水口料) reuse and its cost in the unit margin.
- **Decision route:** ask whether the sole director lives in Thailand (resident = more Thai autonomy; nominee = HQ decides); search DigiWin's internal CRM for the China group's names and brand, and raise a cross-border referral only if it is an account, always through the field owner.
- **Timing:** 4–6 months of implementation, live before trial production. When NOVA back-calculates a signing month that has already passed, keep the rule and treat the window as now.
- Caution (Claude vetting): NOVA called it a "黃金商機"; opened with the capital raise and the group's retailer names and machine count (filings and group facts at the customer, §43); used fear framing on BOI back-tax and retailer chargebacks; built the first-call question on BOI with no certificate found; guessed the director's shareholding; and gave a signing month already past. None adopted. No control-workaround, tax, audit, approval-limit, recording or false-identity advice was offered or adopted.

## §160 — ACP D whose only daily report is "日常联络" (content-free; one data-list contact with a Thai given name and no title, on the GROUP's mail domain, not the director; amount 0; 25 days quiet) at a MICRO YEAR-ONE Thai MOULD-and-INJECTION shop (registered 2024-05, ฿50M registered, ฿12.5M paid in = the 25 per cent minimum, 100 Chinese-held by two holders, one Chinese director signs alone; registry objective moulds, mould parts, fixtures and jigs under a metal-working TSIC; first ~233 days ฿1.85M sales at ~39 gross margin, ฿1.94M loss, ฿2.3M PP&E, no stock, receivables ~249 days of sales; FY2025 not yet public; re-posting injection-technician jobs), self-described Thai branch of a Singapore-invested Chinese precision-mould group (Shanghai 2005, now Jiashan; automotive fuel-system, HVAC/audio-panel and power-tool parts; holds a 2025 patent on an "MES-based precision mould production system", MES brand not named); legal parent unproven; lead in a colleague's 私田 (NOVA live 2026-10-25 · job f07d1a94 · MAINTECH INDUSTRY (THAILAND) run · 南高-商機助手)
- **Grade:** content-free note + untitled, unconfirmed contact = **E (公田 / 長期經營)**, below the SDR's D (behaviour-sequence rule, same as §130/§131/§158/§159). C2 lift facts: reach the Chinese director, general manager or build lead; the customer names a Thai-localisation problem or a head-office visibility problem (Thai output and orders invisible to the HQ MES); the customer agrees to an initial exchange.
- **System pattern for a small overseas year-one subsidiary of a Chinese group with an HQ MES (hypothesis):** HQ ERP/MES extended to the site where it can be, plus a Thai finance and tax layer; the system choice most likely sits at HQ, the Thai director advises on Thai compliance. Phase 1 if a need is stated: Thai finance with VAT/WHT localisation, purchasing, sales and simple stock; production and mould records stay out of phase 1; BOI accounting only if a certificate exists.
- **Size and shape:** worth keeping warm by phone and online (low-cost farming), not worth consultant time now; re-assess when the next filing or the SDR shows machines and orders arriving.
- **Mould-shop question themes (questions, never claims or figures):** how HQ will see the Thai shop's output and order progress next to its own MES; how trial runs (T1-Tx) are costed (hours, trial material) per mould; how engineering changes (ECN) are costed and billed; how customer-owned tools and milestone billing per mould are tracked.
- **Decision route:** the SDR's leader searches DigiWin's internal CRM for the group's China companies; any cross-border step is his decision after that check, always through the field owner, never through Peter.
- Caution (Claude vetting): NOVA scripted the caller as "鼎捷泰國的諾瓦" (false identity) and opened on the company's hiring and "capacity about to open" (OSINT quoted at the customer, capacity not evidenced); built a question on BOI declarations with back-tax fear framing (no BOI record found); used "non-compliance means government fines" as the phase-1 motive; claimed to have coached "many automotive mould makers" (unverified; no Thai automotive-mould case found); gave "80 percent of the decision sits at HQ" (unsourced); and told the field to have the China AM "Call High" at once (only the field owner's leader decides, after his CRM check). None adopted. No control-workaround, tax, audit, approval-limit, recording or false-identity advice was adopted.

## §161 — ACP D whose only daily report is "日常联络" (content-free; contacts linked, no title recorded) at a BOI-PROMOTED (certificate July 2026, gear motors and parts, 5-year CIT holiday), UNDER-CONSTRUCTION Thai plant (registered 2025-05, ฿2M → ฿330M on 2026-06-30, 100 Hong Kong-held, sole director signs alone, FY2025 a ฿0.5M shell) that a PRC- and HK-LISTED micro gear-motor parent names as its 100 subsidiary in its own interim report, where the parent's report itself names SAP, MES, SRM, OA and mould systems; no DigiWin link found; lead in a colleague's 私田 (NOVA live 2026-10-25 · job 6452ffa1 · ZOWWAY (THAILAND) run, parent Shenzhen Zhaowei · 南高-商機助手)
- **Grade:** NOVA live reads it **D (需求探詢)**, not E: a promoted plant under construction must face Thai tax and BOI records soon, so the need is explicit even though the note is empty. This differs from §128 (E for an unbuilt plant with a "long-term follow-up" note and no BOI evidence); the evidenced BOI certificate is the difference. Lift to C2: a build-project lead, an HQ-seconded IT head or the Thai finance head (not admin) acknowledges that the plant must evaluate Thai tax/BOI and operating systems and agrees to a first exchange; and HQ's policy is known (extend SAP everywhere, or allow a local Thai-compliance layer).
- **Entry against a named group SAP-plus-MES stack:** first the Thai finance-and-tax localisation layer together with BOI machine and material records, because both are needed from the first machine import, earlier than any floor system; position as a layer beside the group SAP, never a replacement. Floor systems only later, and only if HQ lets the plant choose. Any Thai purchase must stay visible to and approved by the parent's IT owner.
- **Internal routing:** the field owner asks DigiWin China's unit covering the parent's city whether the parent is an account (any product), who serves it, and what HQ's IT policy for overseas plants is; any introduction to HQ is that unit's and the field owner's decision, never Peter's.
- **Window (NOVA estimate, not evidence):** construction to trial production typically 9–15 months; systems in place about 6 months before trial production; signing window therefore roughly two to three quarters after construction starts. The single fact to learn first: when equipment moves in and trial production starts.
- **Discovery themes (questions only):** how BOI machine-import and material records will be kept and by whom; whether Thai VAT/WHT will be built inside the group SAP or handled by a Thai-localised tool linked to it; whether mould maintenance and machine-utilisation tracking at start-up follow the HQ systems or a lighter local set-up; who leads system and compliance planning, Thai team or HQ.
- Caution (Claude vetting): NOVA asserted that SAP's Thai localisation and BOI development are "極其昂貴且導入期長" and told the field to stress SAP's high customisation cost (unsourced competitor claim; not adopted); claimed DigiWin "行業優勢" in precision injection and moulds (Mould/MES) (unverified; not adopted as a claim); proposed an HQ "Call High … 形成雙向包夾" (pincer framing not adopted; any HQ introduction is DigiWin China's and the field owner's decision); assumed bonded (保稅) imports (BOI privileges beyond the CIT holiday not evidenced); gave trial production 2027 Q2–Q3 and signing 2026 Q4–2027 Q1 (estimates, labelled as such). No control-workaround, tax, audit, approval-limit, recording or false-identity advice was offered.

## §162 — ACP D whose only daily report is "日常联络" (content-free; one contact linked, no title; amount 0; 25 days quiet) at a large, PROFITABLE, 100 THAI-HELD cosmetics and dietary-supplement CONTRACT MANUFACTURER in Phitsanulok (registered 2015, ฿5M capital, five unnamed holders, one Thai director signs alone; revenue ฿65M → ฿1.0B 2021–2025, 2025 net ~14, gross margin 10.8 → 25.0 in one year, stock ~฿162M ≈ 79 days of cost of sales, no dividend) whose own October 2026 job posts name Express (accounting), HumanSoft (HR), "accounting program or Excel" and an outside accounting office, while hiring QC for mixing, purchasing and brand designers in bulk; lead in a colleague's 私田 (NOVA live 2026-10-25 · job c0738599 · BEAUTY COSMET run · 南高-商機助手)
- **Grade:** NOVA reads **D or E** on the behaviour-sequence rule (no customer buying behaviour; a content-free note), even at ฿1B with strong OSINT pain; the skill's OSINT field may still read C2-candidate on the evidence (same split as §33). Lift to C2: direct contact with the signing director or a trusted factory, QC or finance head; agreement that Express plus Excel no longer carries the business; agreement to a discovery visit or first presentation. Never C1 without the signatory.
- **Phase 1 for a Thai cosmetics/supplement OEM outgrowing Express (hypothesis):** DigiWin Workflow ERP tier, standard quick-start scope, no customisation; in scope = stock, purchasing, BOM and formula, batch/lot and expiry, sales and work orders; out of phase 1 = actual costing (standard or estimated cost first) and finance (keep Express for cash and tax filing so the outside accounting office keeps its routine, ERP feeding stock and sales detail). Claude caution: two ledgers for a while; agree the hand-off with the company's accountant, never design around them.
- **Champion profile:** factory manager, QC head or the new purchasing lead; not the outside accountant and not a clerk.
- **OEM discovery themes (questions only):** how brand-driven formula or packaging changes are handled without stranding old packaging and with lots matching the amended Thai FDA notification; where the mixing-section QC and incoming-material records disagree and stop production; what in the Express-plus-Excel set-up would break first if more international brand orders came.
- Caution (Claude vetting): NOVA claimed DigiWin "helped many Thai cosmetics/supplement OEMs" and suggested sharing "Thai local OEM cases" (none found; the references found are Taiwanese 2018 cases 益美化工 1055 and 和康 674); suggested approaching staff via Facebook/LINE OA "as a B2B strategic-cooperation or capacity consultant" (a pretext); used unsourced figures ("10 of stock = ฿16.2M", "3 days to 10 minutes"); quoted filing figures at the owner (§43); speculated about IPO; commented on the director's gender. None adopted. No control-workaround, tax, audit, approval-limit, recording or false-identity advice was adopted.


## §163 — Built, PRE-REVENUE Thai site (registered 2024-10, ฿5M → ฿50M, fully paid by FY2025 by arithmetic; PP&E ~฿1M → ~฿206M and stock ~฿12M in FY2025, zero sales, ~฿199M interest-free current liabilities) held 100 by two Chinese holders, parent UNRESOLVED, no public footprint; registry objective = renting out its OWN factory building (TSIC 68104), latest filing = wholesale of metal parts and pipe caps (TSIC 46622); ACP D whose only report is "日常联络"; contacts = a generic HR address from the DBD list and a Thai 特助 met at a DigiWin seminar (not a director); lead in a colleague's 私田 (NOVA live 2026-10-25 · job df5a0dd4 · PROFESSIONAL PRECISION MANUFACTURING (THAILAND) run · 南高-商機助手)
- **Grade:** contact with no stated pain, timeline or budget = **D (需求探詢)**, same as §124/§127. C2 = a director or the Chinese plant head personally confirms the Thai site will evaluate a system; C1 = the 立案三要項 (dated must-do such as the production start, a formally named project owner, a dated evaluation and go-live plan).
- **Reading "rental objective + wholesale filing + big PP&E + stock, no sales" (new prior):** NOVA reads it as a plant preparing to produce; a pure landlord would not carry stock. Keep it as the likeliest of three (plant / importer stocking for first sales / landlord for an affiliate) and resolve with three questions: build and trial status and first-shipment date; which legal entity invoices, buys material and would sign; domestic sales vs export.
- **Scope by branch:** plant = Workflow phase 1 (purchasing, sales, stock; finance with Thai VAT, WHT, fixed assets; LRP batch planning and basic work orders), phase 2 MOM boards or barcode WMS; trader = purchasing, sales, stock, finance with multi-currency and import-cost allocation, no production; landlord-only = probably NOT a Workflow customer (local finance with fixed assets and lease billing). Windows: plant 3–6 months before trial production; trader ~2 months before the first import; landlord ~1 month before the first rent invoice.
- **Public parent checks the field owner can run (no guessed characters):** ask the contact neutrally which company invested; China's register of overseas-investment filings (境外投資企業（機構）名錄) by the Thai company's English name; the Thai trademark register if a brand appears.
- Caution (Claude vetting): NOVA scripted an opener claiming DigiWin had helped "非常多羅勇的中資工廠建廠" and offered an "ERP 規劃與避坑指南" and a "系統評估檢核表" (unverified claim; assets not known); justified asking for the parent's name with "我們在中國也有許多管帽行業的指標客戶" (unverified); suggested checking the 特助's LinkedIn and watching her WeChat moments for the parent's name (not adopted: no LinkedIn page visits, no monitoring of a private person's social accounts); used fear framing ("事後補帳...極大災難", "無法出貨或無法通過客戶稽核"); raised BOI duty-free handling with no certificate found (question only). None adopted beyond the questions above. No control-workaround, tax, audit, approval-limit, recording, false-identity or direct-contact advice was adopted.

## §164 — ACP D whose only daily report is "展会认识，该司公司预计明年初竣工。长期追踪" (trade-fair contact, title unknown; plant due "early next year"; amount 0; 25 days quiet) at a PRE-PRODUCTION Thai FASTENER company (registered 2025-03, ฿1.38B registered on day one, 100 Hong Kong-held, two directors either signing alone, Bangkok office address, plant site not public, DBD statement figures blank) that a SHANGHAI-LISTED, TAIWANESE-FAMILY-CONTROLLED fastener group names in its audited annual report as a 100 subsidiary set up "to raise overseas market share" after anti-dumping duties cut its exports; the parent's own reports name "深度定制的 ERP、EBS、OA" and say subsidiaries are controlled through OA and ERP; no BOI certificate found (standard 20 tax rate in the parent's notes); no DigiWin link found; lead in a colleague's 私田 (NOVA live 2026-10-25 · job 9f5ad8df · GEM-YEAR INDUSTRIAL run, parent 晋亿实业 601002 · 南高-商機助手)
- **Grade:** NOVA live reads **E (長期經營)**, one notch below the SDR's D: a fair meeting with a contact of unknown rank, no confirmed need and no confirmed willingness to evaluate is not buying behaviour, even with ฿1.38B and a known completion quarter (consistent with §128; contrast §161, where an evidenced BOI certificate made the need explicit and NOVA read D). Lift to C2: a Thai build-team member, the plant's assistant or the finance head holds a first conversation, acknowledges a concern about Thai tax/compliance or about extending the HQ system, and agrees to a first briefing; HQ's policy is known. C1 = the 立案三要項.
- **Entry against a parent's own customised ERP plus EBS:** a Thai finance-and-tax localisation layer beside the group system (VAT, WHT, e-Tax, fixed assets from the build phase), BOI records only if a certificate exists; never a head-on replacement; no presales resources before a real conversation with a Thai-side key contact. Interface claims stay at §22/§81 (report formats, upload files, per-project mapping), never "seamless" API consolidation into the parent's EBS.
- **Fastener start-up discovery themes (questions only, priors not evidence):** unit conversion between weight (wire rod in tonnes/kg) and count (pieces/thousands at shipment) and who owns it; tracking progress and losses at outsourced heat treatment and surface treatment; life and adjustment counts of heading dies and punches at trial production.
- **Routing:** the field owner asks DigiWin China's unit covering the parent's city whether the parent is an account and what its ERP/EBS are; never Peter directly.
- Caution (Claude vetting): NOVA claimed "100% 通過 BOI 審計" and "100% 符合 Revenue Department 申報格式" (overclaims), proposed a "T100 緊固件行業版" (unverified product), promised a "Data Gateway" with seamless XML/API consolidation into EBS (beyond §22), scripted an opener that assumes BOI ("勢必會申請") and quotes the capital to the customer (§43), called the capital "實收" (registered, not verified paid), used fear framing ("帳務極度混亂"), gave unsourced durations (ERP "6~9 個月" for a fastener plant; sign "2026 Q3~Q4") and told the channel head to contact DigiWin China and co-work the colleague's account himself. None adopted (routing left to the field owner). No control-workaround, tax, audit, approval-limit, recording or false-identity advice was offered.

## §165 — ACP D whose only daily report is "拜访过客户，且一直邀请客户参加活动，客户用了行业ERP，暂时没有更换ERP计划" (visited; invited to events; customer runs an INDUSTRY-SPECIFIC ERP and has NO PLAN TO CHANGE; amount 0; 31 days quiet) at a PROFITABLE, 100 PRC-held Thai OFFSET-PRINTED PAPER-PACKAGING plant (registered 2022-12, ฿392.1M fully paid by arithmetic, first full year 2025 ฿293M sales and ฿61.6M net profit, normal 18 tax so no full BOI holiday, year-end receivables about 121 days of sales; self-described Qingdao parent not proved by registry); lead in a colleague's 私田 (NOVA live 2026-10-25 · job 23a2b649 · LETAI PACKAGING (THAILAND) run · 南高-商機助手)
- **Grade:** NOVA live reads **E (長期經營)**: a customer-stated "industry ERP, no plan to change" is satisfaction with the status quo, not buying behaviour, whatever the plant's size or profit. Lift to C2 only on a fact: the customer states a Thai tax / VAT / WHT / e-Tax gap in the Chinese package; HQ (the parent) asks to tighten control of the Thai unit (receivables, stock, job margin); or Thai staff are not really using the system (data kept by hand). C1 = the 立案三要項.
- **Entry against an installed Chinese industry ERP:** never "change your ERP". Side entry only, and only after the customer names the gap: a Thai finance-and-tax localisation layer beside the incumbent (VAT, WHT, e-Tax), or floor data collection and reporting on the presses, die-cutters and foil lines (eMES phase 2 in the TH register; NOVA said "SFC", which the TH product rule replaces); interface claims stay at §22.
- **Paper premium packaging discovery themes (questions only, priors not evidence):** whether Thai tax returns and e-Tax invoices come straight from the Chinese package or via a Thai accountant and spreadsheets; how make-ready time and sheet waste on the big presses and die-cutters are captured (machine or end-of-shift paperwork); whether actual cost and margin per job are visible soon after the job or only at month-end; Thai-language interface and who supports Thai users when the vendor is in China.
- **Routing:** the field owner checks DigiWin China's CRM by the parent's verified Chinese name through the normal cross-region channel (the unit covering the parent's city, or the packaging industry group); never Peter directly, never on a guessed name.
- Caution (Claude vetting): NOVA invented a Chinese legal name for the parent ("青島樂之泰工業有限公司"; not adopted, the name is unverified); used fear framing (tax-audit fines, "引發其對現有系統不足的焦慮", bad-debt risk) and an unsourced loss figure ("幾千銖" per hour of a press); assumed e-Tax becomes mandatory (not verified); told the SDR to open by quoting the DBD revenue at the customer (§43); offered "泰國輔導其他中資包裝廠" cases (unverified) and a "稅務合規檢查表" (unverified asset); said "SFC". None adopted. No control-workaround, tax-avoidance, audit, approval-limit, recording or false-identity advice was offered.

## §166 — ACP D whose daily reports are an event invitation and then "参加线下研讨会，工厂还未建好。日常建联中" (a seminar attendee, title unknown; plant not built; amount 0; 25 days quiet) at a PRE-REVENUE, 100 PRC-held Thai company (registered 2025-03, ฿50M → ฿100M, about ฿50M paid in by FY2025 by arithmetic, PP&E ~฿293M on ~฿289M interest-free short-term liabilities, one Chinese sole-signing director, parent UNRESOLVED) whose name says "Autoparts" but whose registry objective (mining, ore dressing, smelting, ore trading; TSIC 28240) and own job ads (antimony assay lab reconciling supplier and company assays before ore purchase, a factory interpreter, a Chinese-speaking full-cycle accounting supervisor) describe an ANTIMONY ORE BUYING, DRESSING AND SMELTING greenfield; lead in a colleague's 私田 (NOVA live 2026-10-25 · job 8cfe1bba · HQ AUTOPARTS (THAILAND) run · 南高-商機助手)
- **Grade:** NOVA live reads **D (需求探詢)**, not E: real build investment plus live hiring = build-preparation phase; no C1 without system need, budget, go-live date and a decision-maker contact (compare §163 D, §164 E: a seminar or fair touch alone is borderline; sunk PP&E plus hiring tipped it to D). C2 = a conversation with the sole director, the build lead or the new accounting head acknowledging a build/start-up management need (Thai tax filing, reporting to China, purchasing control). C1 = the 立案三要項.
- **Process / ore-smelting greenfield scope fence (new prior, reusable for non-ferrous and other assay-priced process plants):** first cut = Thai finance and tax (bilingual, VAT/WHT), build-phase equipment purchasing and fixed assets, and lot attributes (grade such as Sb content, moisture) as the basis of purchase pricing and receipt. Do NOT promise process-control (DCS/SCADA/OT) integration, and do NOT promise an automatic assay-to-price settlement formula: the customer calculates settlement outside (Excel) and the ERP records the final price against the graded lot.
- **Slow-down signals before presales effort:** factory licence (ร.ง.4) and environmental assessment status unknown; funding source unconfirmed (large interest-free group advances). Ask neutrally through the field owner; never raise as fear.
- **Routing:** the field owner asks the contact neutrally for the investing company and checks DigiWin China's CRM by a VERIFIED name through the normal cross-region channel; never on a guessed Chinese rendering of a Thai-script name, never Peter directly.
- Caution (Claude vetting): NOVA scripted an opener quoting the DBD fixed-asset figure to the director (§43), assumed a BOI duty-exemption write-off (no certificate found), used "非常嚴格" framing, proposed 天眼查/企查查 searches on a guessed Chinese name and an email-domain lookup the job pages did not support, told the team to "立即借力中國團隊 CALL HIGH 總部", speculated about nominee holding and money laundering, and asserted a foreign-business-licence need for mining (unverified). None adopted. No control-workaround, tax, audit, approval-limit, recording or false-identity advice was adopted.

## §167 — ACP D whose only daily report is "客戶財務總：<name> / 电话不通，微信未通过" (the SDR has only the finance head's NAME; the phone did not connect and the WeChat friend request was not accepted; nobody spoken to; amount 0; 31 days quiet) at a RAMPING, 100 PRC-held Thai GENERAL-MACHINERY plant in Pluak Daeng, Rayong (TSIC 28199; registered 2019-08, ฿5M → ฿50M in 2020, about ฿27M paid in by arithmetic; a shell 2019–2022, then PP&E ฿35M → ฿88M → ฿177M in 2023–2025 on ~฿189M interest-free liabilities; first sales year FY2025 ~฿6.5M at a NEGATIVE gross margin, ~฿9.9M loss, stock ~890 days of cost of sales, receivables above a year's sales; one Chinese sole-signing director; product, parent and website unknown; no job ads); lead in a colleague's 私田 (NOVA live 2026-10-25 · job 31cd82e6 · METAK INDUSTRY (THAILAND) run · 南高-商機助手)
- **Grade:** a name with failed phone and WeChat contact = **E (公田 / 長期經營)**, below the SDR's D (same rule as §150, §154: no two-way conversation, no voiced problem = no D). C2 = a two-way conversation in which the finance head or a plant manager acknowledges an operating problem (stock not matching the books, Thai tax filing, cash tied up) and asks about solutions.
- **First cut for a ramping plant with unknown product (reusable):** Workflow ERP finance core (GL, AR, AP, cash) with Thai VAT/WHT; purchasing, sales and stock with receipt/issue/count discipline; work orders and subcontracting with LRP. **Phase-1 fence:** no actual or detailed product costing in year one (BOM, labour and overhead data not reliable yet; costing is phase 2 once stock and work-order records are clean), no APS, no barcode WMS, no group consolidation while the parent's system and rules are unknown; promise the Thai single-plant books only.
- **First questions (neutral, through the field owner):** what the plant makes (complete machines to order vs parts in batches; own machining vs assembly and subcontracting); what system the parent uses and whether the Thai books and stock run on Excel, a Thai package or an outside accountant.
- **Re-entry triggers:** job-board postings for a Thai accountant, warehouse supervisor or IT staff; a registered-capital increase; a published BOI certificate.
- Caution (Claude vetting): NOVA suggested a cold gate visit pitching "財務合規系統" to the guard, routing through unnamed partners (聯通, Chinese banks) and Chinese-chamber WeChat groups, an opener claiming Thai tax audits of new plants were "非常嚴格" in 2025, speculated that the company is "極可能" applying for BOI, set an unsourced numeric work-order close-rate threshold, and generalised about what Chinese finance heads fear. None adopted. No control-workaround, tax, audit, approval-limit, recording, false-identity or direct-contact advice was adopted.

## §168 — ACP D whose only daily report is "发送活动资料给对方先了解" (event material sent one way; recipient and role unrecorded; amount 0; 25 days quiet) at the Thai plant of a PRC-LISTED small-kitchen-appliance ODM (parent's own exchange filing names the Thai company and its registration number; held 90 Singapore / 10 Hong Kong path companies, 100 indirect; ฿700M registered, ~US$50M project, 7 million units a year planned at Nong Lalok, Rayong; FY2024 pre-revenue with ~฿469M PP&E and no bank debt; US customs bills show air fryers and space heaters shipping to US brand owners and online sellers from mid-2025); one Thai director signs without limit, the other only within admin and production limits and for "purchasing against sales orders approved by the shareholder company"; no system named for plant or parent; lead in a colleague's 私田 (NOVA live 2026-10-25 · job bb1035d3 · FUHAODA (THAILAND) run · 南高-商機助手)
- **Grade:** one-way material with no reply and no known contact role = **E (公田 / 長期經營)**, whatever the plant's size (same as §155/§167). C2 = the plant's contact identified by role, a stated problem (ramp, customer audits, reconciliation with headquarters) and a willingness to evaluate or meet.
- **Route for a plant whose purchasing follows parent-approved orders (adopted):** the SDR's leader first asks the DigiWin China colleagues covering the parent's city whether the parent is an account, on which system, and its overseas-plant policy; Peter never contacts DigiWin China directly. Parent on DigiWin (T100 / TOPGP / 易飛): extension led by the China account team at headquarters, DigiWin TH supplying Thai localisation, consulting and support; T100 when headquarters requires the same processes or the plant later needs multi-organisation, consolidation or multi-plant. Parent elsewhere: Thai localisation play, Workflow ERP when the plant decides and needs to go live in three to six months; interfaces by standard APIs plus per-project mapping only (§22).
- **Phase 1 for a ramping small-appliance OEM assembly plant (moulding, stamping, painting, printing, assembly):** core ERP with Thai finance and tax; purchasing, sales and stock; basic work orders and BOM (issue to assembly, finished-goods receipt); basic batch or lot numbers for customer audits. Fence: no APS (moulding and assembly scheduling differ), no shop-floor barcode, MES or WMS, no EDI with customers in phase 1.
- **Reading the signing clause:** a clause tying Thai purchasing to "sales orders approved by the shareholder company" is registry evidence that order approval sits with the parent; check which director the limits bind before concluding that the Thai board cannot sign.
- Caution (Claude vetting): NOVA scripted a congratulatory opener quoting the plant's US shipments (customs data at the customer), "我們服務許多出貨美國（如 Sensio, Select Brands）的家電大廠" (unverified, and names the customer's own buyers from customs data), "確保訂單不會被稽核卡住" (fear framing), a question to the customer about its directors' signing limits (filing detail at the customer), disparagement of SAP/Oracle consultants in Thailand (unsourced), "合併報表一鍵對接" (overpromise), a ฿1.0–1.5M Workflow budget band (unsourced) and a generalisation about Thai and Myanmar workers; it also read one director's limits as binding the whole board. None adopted. No control-workaround, tax, audit, approval-limit, recording, false-identity or direct-contact advice was adopted.

## §169 — ACP D whose only daily report is the finance manager's explicit NO ("用国内用友U8，财务外包泰国一家本地公司 / 财务经理告知不会新购ERP或者更换外包财务公司，暂时不需要，已发资料") at a RAMPING Thai AUTOMOTIVE-MOULD plant (registered 2023-07, ฿500M, Chinese 100, ~฿425M PP&E, first sales FY2025 ~฿6.7M, small loss, no debt) of a SZSE-LISTED mould and checking-fixture maker whose annual report names the Thai subsidiary; sole Thai signatory probably the parent's chairman; the plant advertising an in-house Chinese-speaking accounting and tax officer 24 days after the "no"; lead in a colleague's 私田 (NOVA live 2026-10-25 · job 5ec998c7 · CHAODA EQUIPMENT (THAILAND) run · 南高-商機助手)
- **Grade (reusable):** an explicit "no new ERP, no change of the outsourced accountant, group U8 in use" = **E (公田 / 長期經營)**, not D: no quantified pain, no project team, no timeline. D = the plant itself states a Thai books or tax problem that the group ERP plus an outside firm does not solve (watch for the in-house accountant's arrival); C2 = the plant head or the parent's IT/finance management wants mould-project or floor visibility solved.
- **Positioning beside a group U8 at a make-to-order mould plant (reusable, same shape as §153):** two-tier complement, never replacement. Phase 1 only if asked: Thai statutory layer (VAT, WHT, e-Tax; Thai tax books only, group ledger untouched, entries passed back) and mould-project progress and cost (design, machining, trial, delivery; steel, hours per process and trial cost per mould). Fence: no U8 replacement talk, no rework of HQ master-data rules, no group consolidation. Interface claims per §22.
- **First field-owner checks:** whether DigiWin China has history with the parent (the SDR's leader checks the China CRM, so the two sides do not collide); whether the Thai plant's machines are installed and running.
- Caution (Claude vetting): NOVA proposed courting the newly hired accountant with a congratulation and a "BOI 與稅務申報實務手冊" so that he lobbies HQ for budget past the finance manager who said no ("借刀殺人"; not adopted: works around the customer's own finance chain; the handbook is not known to exist), asserted U8 cannot do Thai filings and that the hire "一定" will suffer (unverified; see §60, §153), used fine-risk fear framing, proposed an HQ-forcing route via DigiWin China ("從總部下達指令…必須配合開門"; left to the field owners' leader), named a module "EMM (Easy Mold Management)" reading NX data and a 凱華模具 case with 95 / 1.2 / 98 figures (neither found in the TW mirror or the Monk cards, 2026-10-25; not usable), and misdescribed TW cases 3341 上昇 and 1120 鴻運模具 (check the case page before quoting any NOVA case summary). Adopted: grade E with lift facts, two-tier positioning, phase-1 scope and fence, the two checks. No control-workaround, tax, recording or false-identity advice was adopted.

## §170 — ACP D whose only daily report is "T100老客，日常邀请参加活动和建联。十月去拜访" (event invitations, visit planned; contacts = three FINANCE people incl. one Thai ERP project lead; amount 0; Thai 累計銷售金額 0, no contract record, contacts filed 新客) at a PRE-REVENUE, BOI-bound Thai drive-shaft / CV-joint plant (registered 2023-07, ฿300M, Singapore 93 / Chinese 7, one Chinese sole signatory; FY2025 PP&E ~฿398M, first stock ~฿17M, no sales) of a private Zhejiang auto-parts group whose OWN website says the Thai ERP phase 1 (purchase-sales-stock, finance, smart logistics, BOI compliance) went live 2026-01-28 via an unnamed "external team" with group IT, six items open (BOM, print templates, field translation, stock count, BOI ledger, run-period KPIs), phase 2 planned = shop-floor dispatch/reporting, PLM, QMS; lead in a colleague's 私田 (NOVA live 2026-10-25 · job 7d7dd54a · HONGLI AUTO PARTS (THAILAND) run · 南高-商機助手)
- **Stage:** event invitations plus a planned visit, finance-only contacts and no Thai evaluation mandate = **D (需求探詢)**, even with a published phase-2 plan. C2 = the SDR meets a plant decision-maker (plant head, production or project lead); C1 = a quantified must-do for phase 2 plus a named phase-2 team and a timetable.
- **Reading "T老客" + a just-live Thai ERP by an "external team":** most likely the Thai plant is a site on the group's mainland T100 (§43, §61, §76), but the go-live may have been done by group IT or a non-DigiWin implementer. Verify before use, through the field owner's leader (who decides whether DigiWin China is asked): whether the group's T100 covers a Thai legal entity and multi-organisation, who implemented Thailand, and who owns phase 2. Claude vetting: an ACP 累計銷售金額 0 + 新客 on the Thai side is consistent with a group-level relationship, not proof of one.
- **Phase-2 order for a ramping automotive drive-shaft plant:** floor reporting with quality traceability (eMES reporting + QMS lot trace) before PLM, because product development usually stays at HQ. Gate first: confirm the ERP of record (§1, §47).
- **Open phase-1 items as the door:** the customer's own BOI-ledger and Thai-translation leftovers are natural DigiWin TH localisation work; never promise to fix phase-1 leftovers free. Claude vetting: offer it as "after-sales care" ONLY if the Thai site is confirmed to run a DigiWin product; otherwise it is a false pretext.
- Caution (Claude vetting): NOVA told the TH channel head to call the DigiWin China Hangzhou / Jiaxing AM himself and run a weekly China–Thailand alignment (not adopted: Peter does not contact DigiWin China; the field owner's leader decides), claimed T100 native modules cannot pass car-maker audits (unverified), generalised that outside implementers do not understand Thai BOI (unverified), scripted questions that presume "T100 的泰文界面" and an IATF audit timetable (not evidenced), used "cannot get the BOI exemption" as the must-do (fear framing), offered unverified Thai auto-parts MES/QMS cases, and speculated that the customer doubts DigiWin's delivery. None may be used. No control-workaround, recording or false-identity advice was adopted.

## §171 — ACP D whose only daily report is "日常联络" (content-free; two seminar contacts with given names only, no title; amount 0; 22 days quiet) at a LARGE, RAMPING Thai export plant (registered 2023-06, ฿5M → ฿2.35B in eight raises, fully paid; FY2025 sales ฿2.26B ×18.5, gross margin ~15, small net loss; year-end stock ~235 days of cost of sales and receivables ~271 days of sales; parent bank guarantees) of a SHENZHEN-LISTED Ningbo disposable-tableware maker whose own interim report says its "digital management platform" now covers the Thai base (ERP brand not public); BOI tax holiday applied; lead in a colleague's 私田 (NOVA live 2026-10-25 · job 37c891aa · HOME RICHFULL TECHNOLOGY (THAILAND) run · 南高-商機助手)
- **Grade:** content-free note + untitled seminar contacts = **E (公田 / 長期經營)**, below the SDR's D, whatever the plant's size (same rule as §160/§163/§168). C2 lift facts: a role-holder reached (planning head, the sole money-signing director or a named project owner); the customer acknowledges a ramp, BOI or HQ-coordination problem and wants a solution; a real exchange has happened.
- **Entry for a Thai plant of a listed PRC group with its own platform (hypothesis):** (A) Thai compliance layer beside the HQ system (BOI machine/material records, Thai VAT/WHT) positioned as "HQ core unchanged"; (B) barcode stock/WMS and simple floor reporting where a newly hired planner fights overstock and shortage. **Stop rule (adopted):** if the field owner learns HQ mandates one ERP instance for Thailand with finance, BOI and production all built by HQ IT and no third-party local system allowed, drop it as "group system, do not chase".
- **Reading high year-end stock and receivables in a ramping US-export disposables plant (hypothesis, ask as process questions):** raw material built ahead of capacity, long ocean transit and long buyer terms; ask how forecasts move from US orders to purchasing and who schedules (Thai team or HQ), never quote the filing at the customer.
- **Route:** the SDR's leader may ask the DigiWin China colleagues covering the parent's city whether the parent is an account, on which ERP, and whether HQ IT for overseas plants is centralised; Peter never contacts DigiWin China.
- Caution (Claude vetting): NOVA asserted HQ ERPs (用友/金蝶/SAP) "完全不懂" Thai BOI/VAT/WHT (unsourced generalisation), that the plant's scheduling is "極度混亂" (speculation), named Costco and Walmart as likely buyers (not evidenced), and scripted openers that quote the plant's hiring and BOI status back at the contact (OSINT at the customer). None adopted. No control-workaround, tax, audit, approval-limit, recording, false-identity or direct-contact advice was offered.

## §172 — ACP D whose daily reports are an event invitation ("邀约814活动") and then "回访BOI活动" (a follow-up call after a BOI-themed event; no need, amount, scope or date recorded; 37 days quiet) at a RAMPING, FIRST-SALES-YEAR Thai EV e-DRIVE plant (registered 2024-04, ฿5M → ฿200M, about ฿146M paid in by arithmetic; FY2025 main revenue ~฿1.2M against ~฿7.2M cost of sales, net loss ~฿25.4M, stock ~฿13.5M ≈ 1.9× cost of sales, PP&E ~฿90M, no bank debt; Hong Kong 100, two Chinese directors either signing alone) that a SZSE-LISTED motor group (H-share application filed 2025-09) names in its interim report as a 100 indirect subsidiary and calls its first overseas e-drive base, supplying a Thai-built Chinese EV; three contacts (a VP met by networking, a production "經理" whose name matches a registered director, an import-export clerk); no system named for plant or parent; lead in a colleague's 私田 (NOVA live 2026-10-25 · job 00b2585f · SHANGHAI ELECTRIC DRIVE (THAILAND) run · 南高-商機助手)
- **Stage:** event invitation plus a follow-up call, no stated pain and no evaluation = **D (需求探詢)**, whatever the parent's size. C2 = a meeting with the production manager or VP in which a car-maker audit/traceability concern or a BOI write-off workload is voiced and the customer agrees to a current-state review; C1 = the 立案三要項 (a quantified must-do with a date, a named project contact, a go-live timetable).
- **Order of entry for a listed group's first overseas plant (adopted as hypothesis):** (1) ask first whether HQ mandates its own ERP template for Thailand or lets the plant choose; (2) if HQ's system stays, the Thai layer beside it (BOI material/machinery records, Thai tax and statutory books), interfaced per project (§22, §142); (3) shop-floor traceability for a car-maker supplier second; (4) a whole local ERP only if HQ delegates the choice. Stop rule as §171: one HQ instance with BOI and production built by HQ IT and no third-party local system = do not chase.
- **Contact choice:** the production contact first when his name matches a registry director (verify identity politely, never by quoting the registry at him); the VP second; the import-export clerk is the BOI-paperwork witness, not a decision-maker.
- **Reading a first-year e-drive plant whose filing shows tiny sales against a public "mass production" claim:** ask, never assert, how Thai-built units are invoiced (to the car maker, through a group trading company, or as tolling), because it decides where order, stock and cost data live.
- Caution (Claude vetting): NOVA asserted that a group SAP "根本無法處理" Thai BOI reporting (unsourced; the group's ERP is not even known), scripted "我們在泰國服務非常多中資上市車配企業" and "新能源車三電豐富的車規級導入經驗" (no verified e-drive case in the TW mirror or Monk cards), used recall costs "千萬級起跳" and "避免影響出貨" as fear framing, presumed a Changan "一機一碼" requirement and EDI link (not evidenced), and treated the production manager as a confirmed director (name match only). None adopted. No control-workaround, tax, audit, approval-limit, recording, false-identity or direct-contact advice was offered.

## §173 — ACP D whose only daily report is "日常联络" (content-free; one data-list/networking 業務 經理, not a director; amount 0; 22 days quiet) at a MICRO year-two Thai-Chinese PUMP company (registered 2024-08, ฿5M registered = the skill's DQ line, ~฿2.5M paid in, Thai 51 / Chinese 49 across three holders, one Thai and one Chinese director, either signs alone; registry objective industrial and agricultural pumps under TSIC 28131; first full year FY2025 ~฿5.6M sales at ~13.6 gross, small profit, stock ~1.66x a year's cost of sales, ~฿10.2M almost interest-free current liabilities, only ~฿1.1M of equipment); no website, job post, BOI or press; parent UNRESOLVED; lead in a colleague's 私田 (NOVA live 2026-10-25 · job ea8f755c · JRB PUMPS INDUSTRY GROUP (THAILAND) run · 南高-商機助手)
- **Grade:** content-free note + a non-signatory sales contact = **E (公田 / 長期經營)**, below the SDR's D (behaviour-sequence rule, same as §130/§158/§159/§160). C2 lift facts: reach the Chinese director or the principal's decision-maker; he names a Thai operating problem himself and shows intent to change.
- **Size and shape:** not worth consultant time on the Thai entity alone. If a need is ever stated, phase 1 = standard cloud purchase-sales-stock plus a simple assembly BOM (kit/de-kit); no customisation, no full ERP modules.
- **Pump assembler/reseller question themes (questions only):** where parts and finished pumps come from and the order-to-shelf lead time; how stock answers an urgent spec change; whether Ban Phaeo does final assembly and testing and how model BOMs are kept (paper or system); whether Thai sales targets and purchase budgets are decided locally or approved by a China head office, and who would evaluate a system.
- **Finding the principal:** registry first (paid บอจ.5 names the holders, Peter's call); public trademark records for the brand name are a legitimate free check; the DigiWin China account check goes through the field owner's leader once a principal is named, never through Peter.
- Caution (Claude vetting): NOVA called it a "偽商機" with "財務暴雷預警", "現金流極度吃緊" and "收款風險極高" (the company is profitable and its liabilities are interest-free; year-one stocking is likelier; not adopted as fact); stated "這根本不是製造業" as fact (kept as a question); proposed reverse-guessing the Chinese holders' names in pinyin on 天眼查/企查查 (holders are not public; personal-name speculation, not adopted) and paid customs-data platforms (no paid sources; not adopted); proposed high up-front payment terms (presumes distress; not adopted); wrote the next-call questions in the first person "我們在泰國" (reworded so the caller never speaks as the company); routed the parent check to a DigiWin China "中資出海專案小組" directly (only via the field owner's leader). No control-workaround, tax, audit, approval-limit, recording or false-identity advice was adopted.

## §174 — Pre-revenue Thai plant of a PRC private AFTERMARKET steering-rack exporter, set up to move US-bound output out of China, whose FIRST US Customs origin ruling came back CHINA (core rack/pinion/valve still imported); group already claims ERP+MES+WMS+PLM (vendor unknown); cold, Peter-sourced, off his Thai-local mandate (NOVA live 2026-10-27 · job c8d0bbdd · AIERFU (Thailand) run · 南高-商機助手)
- **Grade:** no contact at all = **E (公田 / 長期經營)** for the pipeline, however strong the public pain (behaviour-sequence rule). The OSINT recommendation may still say C2-candidate (concrete latent need evidenced by the ruling); keep the two fields separate. Lift facts: (1) a signatory engages and confirms intent on the Thai IT plan and the origin problem; (2) the parent link and the IT decision chain (HQ-controlled vs Thai-autonomous) are confirmed; (3) a dated plan for in-country rack/pinion machining and first US shipments, from which a go-live is back-calculated.
- **Wedge, two hands:** signatory level = US-origin evidence (per-lot BOM + routing record showing which parts were made in Thailand from what material) against the Section 301 gap; finance level = BOI duty-free parts control + TFRS vs CAS dual reporting for the new accountant.
- **Phase 1:** Workflow ERP + Thai localisation (and BOI where promoted) + BOM + lot control + work orders; **no eMES in phase 1** for a small pre-revenue plant. Claude vetting: NOVA wrote "簡易製程管制 (SFT/WIP)"; house rule = worker/per-piece shop-floor data is eMES phase 2, never SFT/SFC, so phrase phase 1 as work orders with lot recording.
- **Parent on 用友/金蝶 (hypothesis):** position the Thai plant as an overseas compliance-and-manufacturing node; DigiWin runs Thai tax, BOI and production records locally and posts finance vouchers back to the group system through an interface; never ask HQ to replace its system.
- **Ownership:** a PRC-outbound plant belongs to the TH Chinese-desk / direct team; Peter (Head of Distributor) = lead provider, registers it in ACP after checking for a colleague's booking, shares credit by the internal rule, and does not visit. Parent-system check: DigiWin China East-China region (NOVA: Anhui usually under Hefei or Nanjing; unverified) via the CRM.
- **Contract size:** design phase 1 below the local signing limit when the registry shows a second director with a capped authority (here ฿3M), so it can be signed in Thailand once HQ agrees.
- Caution (Claude vetting): NOVA called the capped second director the "泰國總經理" (registry shows only "director") and assumed the main signatory sits in China (he signed the CBP request from the Thai site); it asserted that 用友/金蝶 Thai localisation and local consulting are "遠不及鼎新" (unverified, never to be said to a customer). None adopted as fact.

## §175 — Cold PRC-private-parent plant UNDER A GOVERNMENT STOP ORDER (buried hazardous foundry waste, operating without start notification), single dominant US appliance OEM customer, first revenue year (NOVA live 2026-10-27 · job a03b977d · Jinjiale (Thailand) run · 南高-商機助手)
- **Stage:** no contact = **E in CRM, flagged C2-candidate** — a regulator stop order plus a single-customer dependency is a textbook hidden pain (Lv.2–3): the owner knows the problem, not how to prove improvement. Matches the skill's OSINT rule (C2-candidate needs concrete pain evidence; D needs contact).
- **Timing:** NOVA says **contact now** ("雪中送炭" window) and sell "restart compliance + customer-audit defence" (material/waste flow records, lot trace), NOT an ERP. Claude vetting: keep the framing, but (a) whether DigiWin approaches a company under criminal prosecution for hazardous dumping is a management call; (b) never promise a regulatory, customer-audit or origin outcome — the system supplies records that SUPPORT filings; (c) NOVA's "免死金牌", "百分之百符合" and "Electrolux EHS audit is coming" are overclaims / unevidenced — never to a customer.
- **Ownership:** 100% PRC-held → **TH Chinese direct-sales team** owns the lead, routed with DigiWin China's South China unit (Guangzhou/Zhaoqing) for the parent's CRM and ERP check; Peter's role = local delivery resources (Thai/Burmese-speaking consultants) and Thai partner referrals (accountants, environmental-filing agents). Consistent with the AIERFU ruling the same day.
- **Phase 1 (NOVA):** stock + lot traceability (raw material, waste sand, WIP, finished goods), shop-floor work orders and production reporting, Thai finance/tax (tax invoices, AR). NOVA leaned **T100** for two-stage foundry + enamel at a planned ~฿1.8B output. Claude vetting: KB §4 bands on ACTUAL revenue (฿19M) do not support T100 yet; keep the tier open for /select-t100-vs-workflow; melt/heat-to-pack traceability belongs to eMES phase 2 on a lot-controlled ERP base.
- **If the parent runs Kingdee/Yonyou:** dual track — HQ keeps consolidation, Thai plant on DigiWin with an API/finance link. Claude vetting: NOVA's "Kingdee/Yonyou lack Thai delivery" is CONTRADICTED by evidence (Kingdee via SourceCode, Haers TH kickoff 2026-06-30) — never say it.
- **Hygiene:** NOVA misnamed the company ("金安樂") and half-romanised a director — re-check every proper noun it returns.

## §52 — Thai plant of a Malaysian (Penang) precision-engineering group, two Thai companies on one site, no named system, lead via a distributor with eMES-only scope (NOVA live 2026-11-03 · job 13f086ff · QLT Technology (Thailand) run 2 · 南高-商機助手)
- **Product:** Workflow iGP phase 1 = core (sales, purchasing, stock, AP/AR, GL) + BOM and engineering-change control + multi-company for the two Thai companies; eMES phase 2, three to six months after the ERP base (part numbers, BOM, stock, work orders) is stable. "MES without an ERP cost base is a house on sand."
- **Structure:** one deal, both Thai companies, signed through the cash-rich main entity (matches KB §7).
- **Stage:** D after the first meeting; C2 when the production-side host accepts the design-change and cost diagnosis and brings a director in.
- **Authority:** the final decision sits with the group MD in the parent country. The non-director host is the sponsor: make him the internal hero, never bypass him, and equip him with a findings note for head office.
- **Lead ownership:** a distributor-brought lead stays with the Thai channel. Protect the distributor's eMES position and present ERP + eMES as one package.
- **Proof:** the closest-country, same-trade case first (a Malaysian precision-parts maker), then an overseas precision plant of a regional group.
- **Residual rulings:** raise customer audits (traceability, CMM records) only as a question; never raise a sister company's property deal or loan at a first visit; refer to the group's showcase customers as "world-class customers", never by name.
- **Claude vetting:** NOVA embellished a case ("Penang-listed … lot traceability") beyond what the case page says, and wrote a customer name and the prospect's own margin figure into visit questions. Both were overridden; case-name hygiene applies.

## §176 — Inbound Thai family made-to-order installer (imported components, ~฿155M, home-built ERP + one programmer, owner asks "WMS first on the old system"), three-entity family group, four sibling directors signing in pairs, mother the informal decider (NOVA live 2026-11-04 · jobs 39e25ae0 + a8e35070 · Sang Thong Canvas Awning run · 南高-商機助手)
- **Stage:** an owner-initiated call + a board presentation date + an indicative price heard and accepted is still **C2** until the 立案三要項 exist: a quantified must-change reason agreed by the board, a named project team/PM, a dated go-live (and competition known).
- **Product / sequence:** Workflow iGP; the PRODUCTION entity first on its own contract; rental and trading sisters = phase 2. **Refuse "WMS first on the old in-house ERP"** — no API, no one to build one, WMS becomes an island and double posting starts; ERP stock + purchasing + MO costing first, barcode WMS about a month after stock go-live. Do not design the customer's physical warehouse layout (advise storage logic only).
- **Pain-฿:** lead with the stock-release question on the entity's OWN inventory from DBD, never an owner-remembered or group-summed figure; one-off cash, not yearly savings. Frame an early indicative price as a "standard-package estimate", followed by a short diagnostic before the formal proposal.
- **Lead ownership:** an inbound that already trusts the person who answered stays with that person (handing it to a distributor reads as "being passed on").
- **Family authority:** when press says a parent (not a registered director) still takes the big decisions, treat that parent as the invisible decider with a de facto veto — but do NOT invite them to the first board presentation (the second generation feels over-ruled; the last card is spent); honour the founders / family constitution in the deck; invite later (around B) as the family's strategy adviser. Get attendee names, their areas and the champion's full name through a "so we can tailor the presentation" request. Stay neutral between siblings until the division of roles is known.
- **Hidden objector:** the sole in-house programmer of the old system — position the change as freeing him.
- Claude vetting: NOVA mixed a sister company's inventory and fixed assets into an "assets under management" pitch and suggested splitting the first deck half rental — reject when the rental sister has its own system project and the owner asked for production first; NOVA's uncited benchmark figures (industry inventory days, extra-labour ฿) stay internal.

## §177 — Large profitable Thai food EXPORTER of a Taiwan-LISTED group (rice crackers / baby rusks / noodles / ready rice, ~฿4.25bn, exports >90 %, private label + baby-food brands), entrenched ORACLE ERP with an in-house team, automated warehouse in the next 3-year capex plan; cold, off Peter's Thai-local mandate (NOVA live 2026-11-06 · jobs f2ef3d16 + 8cded58b + 06f17229 · Namchow (Thailand) run · 南高-商機助手)
- **Customer-claim hygiene (new):** NOVA first said the Thai plant was a Digiwin T100 customer since 2018–19 (f2ef3d16), then retracted ("混淆了大陸南僑與泰南僑", 8cded58b), then asserted the PRC-listed sister is a 鼎捷 customer as "真實確鑿之合作紀錄，非推測" (06f17229) — still with no project, customer number or contact; nothing in the TW mirror, 就享知案例, digiwin.com or Baidu. Rule: a NOVA statement that a company IS / WAS a Digiwin customer is a hypothesis until a contract/CRM record or the account owner confirms it (same as case-name hygiene). Never let a rep use it as a "trust chip" with the customer unverified.
- **Product order when an automated warehouse (AS/RS) is coming:** **WMS first, eMES second.** The warehouse build is irreversible and the equipment vendor bundles a location-only WMS once its contract is signed; Digiwin's slot = the WMS brain (lot, FEFO/shelf life, market label, container plan) between Oracle/SAP and the vendor's WCS. eMES follows once materials and goods carry barcodes. Over an entrenched Oracle/SAP: never displacement (§18/§22/§72).
- **Pain order for a margin drop that is mostly FX:** lead with stock age / near-expiry exposure (1–2 % of inventory a year "conservative" for food export), then lot-evidence / recall as zero-tolerance insurance for baby-food co-packing; margin only as a senior opener, never a promise.
- **Lead ownership (100 % Taiwanese, listed parent, decisions in Taipei):** TW 跨國服務處 / 台商 team leads Taipei; the Thai rep is the plant-side relationship; ACP check first; commission split by the cross-border rule.
- Claude vetting (not adopted): "成熟的 Oracle 標準對接接口" (breaks §22); a ฿2.5bn figure for the warehouse (it was the whole 3-year plan); "capacity will double" (unsupported); "1–2 % hidden profit ≈ ฿20–40M" (inconsistent, a promise); handing the GM a pain sheet built from his own filing (§43); "設備商不懂食品" (disparaging a likely partner).

## §72 — Lead passed by a PROSPECTIVE distributor (not yet signed) to the channel head, target a Thai family maker never contacted: channel owns it, register as partner-referred, joint first visit, the lead is the partner's litmus test (NOVA live 2026-11-09 · job aca66bd7 · Sangroong Group run · 南高-商機助手)
- **Ownership:** a lead brought by a partner candidate belongs to the CHANNEL, never to direct sales, even before the partner signs; handing it to direct ends that partner's referrals.
- **Protection:** register it as partner-referred ("Source: Partner <name> (pending agreement)") with a protection period; a simple single-project referral note before the visit, naming roles (partner opens the door and holds the relationship; Digiwin plans and delivers). Commission and CRM blocking are company process — the channel head decides, nothing promised in the field.
- **Stage:** a referrer does not lift the stage; no contact and no buying behaviour = E.
- **Visit:** go together; the partner opens and vouches, Digiwin leads; an untrained partner never answers product or technical questions. No architecture or price at the first visit (avoid free consulting).
- **Pre-visit prep with the partner (30 min):** whom they know and how well (management or operations); whether the target expects Digiwin and what they expect to discuss; whether the target raised a problem or the partner pitched it.
- **Litmus test for the partner:** can they reach a decision-maker rather than an operations contact; how warmly the target treats them; do they prepare and follow up.
- Claude vetting on the same run: NOVA romanised a Thai surname three times, invented a case result, used uncited anchor numbers and put the target's own DBD figures into questions — all overridden (see the dossier's NOVA block).
